 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Handles all GET requests. Used for fetching data.
 * v9.0: every case goes through createHandlerResponse (success: false + status: 'error' when the handler
 * reports a failure - before, doGet wrapped failures as success: true), the default case is loud, and
 * getWorkflowSteps / getSystemHealth are routed (the frontend sends both by GET).
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the GET request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - A JSON response.
 */
function doGet(e) {
    // Safe parameter access with fallback
    const params = e && e.parameter ? e.parameter : {};
    const action = params.action || 'ping'; // Default to 'ping' if no action

    try {
      // Log for debugging
      console.log('doGet called with params:', params);

      // Handle the request based on action
      switch(action) {
        case 'ping':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            message: 'API connection successful',
            serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            responseTime: 150,
            version: API_VERSION
          });

        case 'getCompanies':
          return createHandlerResponse(getCompanies(params));

        case 'getCompany':
          return createHandlerResponse(getCompany(params.companyId));

        case 'getRoles':
          return createHandlerResponse(getRoles(params));

        case 'getDropdownLists':
          return createHandlerResponse(getDropdownLists(params));

        case 'getDropdownOptions':
          return createHandlerResponse(getDropdownOptions(params.listId));

        case 'getTicketTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getTicketTypes(params));

        case 'getTickets':
          return createHandlerResponse(getTickets(params));

        case 'getUserPreferences':
          if (!params.user_id) {
            throw new Error('User ID is required for getting preferences');
          }
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserPreferences(params.user_id));

        case 'getSLAPerformanceByCompany':
          return createHandlerResponse(getSLAPerformanceByCompany(params.company_id || null));

        case 'getSLAPerformanceByTicketType':
          return createHandlerResponse(getSLAPerformanceByTicketTypeAndCompany(params.ticket_type_id || null, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getGlobalSLAPerformance':
          return createHandlerResponse(getGlobalSLAPerformance());

        // Phase 10.0: User Management GET endpoints (v9.0: include_inactive for the admin lists)
        case 'getUserProfileTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserProfileTypes(params.company_id || null, String(params.include_inactive) === 'true'));

        case 'getRoleTypes':
          return createHandlerResponse(getRoleTypes(params.company_id || null, String(params.include_inactive) === 'true'));

        case 'getUserProfiles':
          return createHandlerResponse(getUserProfiles(params.company_id || null));

        case 'getCustomFields':
          // v9.1 (review R-07): the frontend sends ticket_type_id or entityTypeId / entityCategory
          return createDataResponse(getCustomFields(
            params.entity_type_id || params.entityTypeId || params.ticket_type_id || null,
            params.entity_category || params.entityCategory || 'ticket'));

        // v9.0: sent by GET from src/api/googleSheet.js readActions
        case 'getWorkflowSteps':
          return createDataResponse(getWorkflowSteps(params.ticket_type_id || null, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getSystemHealth':
          return createDataResponse(getSystemHealth());

        // v9.1 reads (src/api/googleSheet.js readActions)
        case 'getNextTicketNumber':
          return createDataResponse(peekTicketNumber(params.company_id, params.ticket_type_id));

        case 'getTicketType':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketTypeById(params.ticket_type_id || params.id));

        case 'getCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(getCustomFieldById(params.field_id || params.id));

        case 'getTicketLinks':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketLinksAction(params));

        case 'validateTicketDependencies':
          return createDataResponse(validateTicketDependencies());

        case 'checkCompanyLockStatus':
          return createDataResponse(checkCompanyLockStatus(params.id || params.company_id));

        case 'getCommentRequirements':
          return createDataResponse(getCommentRequirements(params.ticket_type_id, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getTicketHistory':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketHistory(params.ticket_id || params.id));

        case 'getTicketsForApproval':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketsForApproval(params.user_id));

        // v9.2 reads
        case 'getStepConditions':
          return createDataResponse(getStepConditions(params.step_id || params.id));

        case 'getReportConfig':
          return createDataResponse(getReportConfig(params.ticket_type_id, params.company_id || null));

        case 'getReportData':
          return createDataResponse(getReportData(params));

        case 'getAdminActionLogs':
          return createDataResponse(getAdminActionLogs(params));

        case 'getTicketActionLogs':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketActionLogs(params));

        // v9.3 reads
        case 'getDropdownCompanyAssignments':
          return createDataResponse(getDropdownCompanyAssignments(params.dropdown_list_id || params.list_id));

        case 'getCompanySLARules':
          return createDataResponse(getCompanySLARules(params.company_id, params.ticket_type_id || null));

        case 'getTags':
          return createDataResponse(getTagsAction(params));

        case 'getTicketTags':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketTagsAction(params.ticket_id));

        case 'searchTicketsByTags':
          return createDataResponse(searchTicketsByTagsAction(params));

        case 'getTicketShares':
          return createDataResponse(getTicketSharesAction(params.ticket_id));

        case 'getSharedTickets':
          return createDataResponse(getSharedTicketsAction(params.user_id));

        default:
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: false,
            status: 'error',
            error: 'Unknown action: ' + action
          });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('doGet Error:', error);
      return createJsonResponse({
        success: false,
        status: 'error',
        error: error.toString()
      });
    }
}

/**
 * Handles all POST requests. Used for creating or updating data.
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the POST request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - A JSON response.
 */
function doPost(e) {
    // Safe parameter access
    const postData = e && e.postData ? e.postData.contents : '{}';

    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(postData);
      const action = data.action || 'ping';

      console.log('doPost called with action:', action, 'data:', data);

      // v8.3 (CR-03): writes hold the script lock so rebuildAllTablesApply() can't rewrite a tab under them
      if (POST_READ_ONLY_ACTIONS.includes(action)) {
        return routePostAction(action, data);
      }
      return withScriptLock(() => routePostAction(action, data));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('doPost Error:', error);
      return createJsonResponse({
        success: false,
        status: 'error',
        error: error.toString()
      });
    }
}

/**
 * doPost actions that only read - they skip the script lock (A5: never list an action that writes).
 */
const POST_READ_ONLY_ACTIONS = ['ping', 'getCompanies', 'getTickets', 'checkDropdownPersistence',
  // v9.0 reads
  'canUserApprove', 'getStepApprovals', 'getStepApprovers', 'getWorkflowStep', 'getTicket',
  'getUsers', 'getUserProfile', 'getUserRoles', 'getUserGeneralRole',
  // v9.3: read-only, sent by POST because its ticket_type_ids list goes in the body
  'exportConfiguration'];

/**
 * Route one doPost action. Split out of doPost in v8.3 so writes run inside withScriptLock().
 * Errors are thrown to doPost, which returns them as { success: false }.
 * @param {string} action - Action name
 * @param {Object} data - Parsed request body
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} JSON response
 */
function routePostAction(action, data) {
      // Handle the request based on action
      switch(action) {
        case 'ping':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            message: 'API connection successful',
            serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            responseTime: 150,
            version: API_VERSION
          });

        case 'getCompanies':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: getCompanies()
          });

        case 'createCompany':
          // Handle both payload formats: { action, payload } or { action, ...payload }
          const companyData = data.payload || { name: data.name, code: data.code };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createCompany(companyData)
          });

        case 'updateCompany':
          const updateCompanyData = data.payload || { id: data.id, name: data.name, code: data.code, user_id: data.user_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateCompany(updateCompanyData)
          });

        case 'deleteCompany':
          const deactivationData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!deactivationData.id) {
            throw new Error('Company ID is required for deactivation');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: deactivateCompany(deactivationData.id, deactivationData.reason)
          });

        case 'reactivateCompany':
          const reactivationData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id
          };
          if (!reactivationData.id) {
            throw new Error('Company ID is required for reactivation');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: reactivateCompany(reactivationData.id)
          });

        case 'createRole':
          const roleData = data.payload || { name: data.name, company_id: data.company_id, role_type_id: data.role_type_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createRole(roleData)
          });

        case 'updateRole':
          const updateRoleData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id, name: data.name, company_id: data.company_id,
            role_type_id: data.role_type_id, is_active: data.is_active
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateRole(updateRoleData)
          });

        case 'deleteRole':
          const roleDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!roleDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Role ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateRole() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deleteRole(roleDeletionData.id, roleDeletionData.reason));

        case 'createDropdownList':
          // Simplified payload handling to match working company creation pattern
          const dropdownData = data.payload || {
            name: data.name,
            description: data.description || '',
            company_id: data.company_id || null,
            options: data.options || []
          };

          Logger.log('=== SIMPLIFIED DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUG ===');
          Logger.log('🔍 Raw request data keys:', Object.keys(data));
          Logger.log('📥 Final extracted payload:', JSON.stringify(dropdownData, null, 2));

          // Use the debug version for comprehensive validation and logging
          const creationResult = createDropdownListWithDebug(dropdownData);

          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: creationResult
          });

        case 'updateDropdownList':
          const updateDropdownData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            name: data.name,
            description: data.description,
            company_id: data.company_id,
            options: data.options
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateDropdownList(updateDropdownData)
          });

        case 'deleteDropdownList':
          const dropdownDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!dropdownDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Dropdown list ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateDropdownList() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS, dropdownDeletionData.id, dropdownDeletionData.reason));

        case 'createTicketType':
          const ticketTypeData = data.payload || { name: data.name, code: data.code, description: data.description, transaction_id: data.transaction_id, require_attachment_on_create: data.require_attachment_on_create, company_id: data.company_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createTicketType(ticketTypeData)
          });

        case 'updateTicketType':
          const updateTicketTypeData = data.payload || { id: data.id, name: data.name, code: data.code, description: data.description, transaction_id: data.transaction_id, require_attachment_on_create: data.require_attachment_on_create, company_id: data.company_id };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateTicketType(updateTicketTypeData)
          });

        case 'deleteTicketType':
          const ticketTypeDeletionData = data.payload || {
            id: data.id,
            reason: data.reason || 'admin_request'
          };
          if (!ticketTypeDeletionData.id) {
            throw new Error('Ticket type ID is required for deletion');
          }
          // v9.0: deactivateTicketType() never existed - soft delete through deactivateRecord
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeDeletionData.id, ticketTypeDeletionData.reason));

        case 'getTickets':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: getTickets(data)
          });

        case 'createTicket':
          // v9.1: keep description / priority / customData (the old list dropped them)
          const ticketData = data.payload || pickDefined(data, ['title', 'description', 'priority', 'ticket_type_id',
            'requester_id', 'company_id', 'customData', 'custom_fields']);
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: createTicket(ticketData)
          });

        case 'recordLogin':
          const loginData = data.payload || {
            userId: data.userId || data.user_id,
            email: data.email,
            ipAddress: data.ipAddress
          };
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: logLogin(loginData)
          });

        case 'updateUserPreferences':
          const userPreferencesData = data.payload || {
            user_id: data.user_id,
            preferences: data.preferences
          };
          if (!userPreferencesData.user_id) {
            throw new Error('User ID is required for updating preferences');
          }
          if (!userPreferencesData.preferences) {
            throw new Error('Preferences data is required');
          }
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: updateUserPreferences(userPreferencesData.user_id, userPreferencesData.preferences)
          });

        case 'testMinimalDropdownCreate':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: testMinimalDropdownCreate()
          });

        case 'checkDropdownPersistence':
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: true,
            data: checkDropdownPersistence()
          });

        // Phase 10.0: User Management POST endpoints
        // v8.3 (CR-07): these handlers return { status: 'error' } instead of throwing - createHandlerResponse
        // turns that into success:false. The frontend sends user_profile_type_id / role_type_id, not id.
        case 'createUserProfileType':
          const userProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createUserProfileType(userProfileTypeData));

        case 'updateUserProfileType':
          const updateUserProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateUserProfileType(
            updateUserProfileTypeData.id || updateUserProfileTypeData.user_profile_type_id,
            updateUserProfileTypeData
          ));

        case 'deleteUserProfileType':
          const deleteUserProfileTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(
            SHEETS.USER_PROFILE_TYPES,
            deleteUserProfileTypeData.id || deleteUserProfileTypeData.user_profile_type_id,
            deleteUserProfileTypeData.reason || 'admin_request'
          ));

        case 'createRoleType':
          const roleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createRoleType(roleTypeData));

        case 'updateRoleType':
          const updateRoleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateRoleType(
            updateRoleTypeData.id || updateRoleTypeData.role_type_id,
            updateRoleTypeData
          ));

        case 'deleteRoleType':
          const deleteRoleTypeData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(deactivateRecord(
            SHEETS.ROLE_TYPES,
            deleteRoleTypeData.id || deleteRoleTypeData.role_type_id,
            deleteRoleTypeData.reason || 'admin_request'
          ));

        case 'createUserProfile':
          const userProfileData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createUserProfile(userProfileData));

        case 'updateUserProfile':
          const updateUserProfileData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(updateUserProfile(updateUserProfileData.user_id, updateUserProfileData));

        case 'createCustomField':
          const customFieldData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(createCustomField(customFieldData.entity_type_id, customFieldData, customFieldData.entity_category || 'ticket'));

        case 'setCustomFieldValue':
          const customFieldValueData = data.payload || data;
          return createHandlerResponse(setCustomFieldValue(customFieldValueData.entity_id, customFieldValueData.field_id, customFieldValueData.value, customFieldValueData.entity_category || 'ticket'));

        // User Management Setup endpoints (v8.3 (CR-05): safe to repeat - never clears data, seeds test data once)
        case 'initializeUserManagementTables':
          return createHandlerResponse(initializeUserManagementTables());

        case 'completeUserManagementSetup':
          return createHandlerResponse(completeUserManagementSetup());

        // v9.0: approval path. Keys per src/api/googleSheet.js (A7); responses unwrapped (createDataResponse)
        // so response.data is what the frontend method returns.
        case 'canUserApprove':
          return createDataResponse(canUserApproveAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'submitStepApproval':
          return createDataResponse(submitStepApproval(data.payload || data));

        case 'getStepApprovals':
          const stepApprovalsQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getStepApprovals(stepApprovalsQuery.ticket_id, stepApprovalsQuery.step_id));

        case 'getStepApprovers':
          const stepApproversQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getStepApprovers(stepApproversQuery.step_id));

        case 'setStepApprovers':
          const stepApproversData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(setStepApprovers(stepApproversData.step_id, stepApproversData.role_ids));

        case 'getWorkflowStep':
          const workflowStepQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getWorkflowStepById(workflowStepQuery.step_id || workflowStepQuery.id));

        case 'getTicket':
          const ticketQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getTicketById(ticketQuery.ticketId || ticketQuery.ticket_id || ticketQuery.id));

        case 'updateTicket':
          const ticketUpdateData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateTicketWithValidation(ticketUpdateData.id || ticketUpdateData.ticket_id, ticketUpdateData));

        case 'updateTicketStatus':
          return createDataResponse(updateTicketStatusAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'getUsers':
          const usersQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUsersWithAccess(usersQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'getUserProfile':
          const userProfileQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserProfileWithAccess(userProfileQuery.userId || userProfileQuery.user_id));

        case 'getUserRoles':
          const userRolesQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserRoleList(userRolesQuery.userId || userRolesQuery.user_id, userRolesQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'getUserGeneralRole':
          const generalRoleQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getUserGeneralRole(generalRoleQuery.userId || generalRoleQuery.user_id, generalRoleQuery.company_id || null));

        case 'assignUserRole':
          return createDataResponse(assignUserRoleAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'removeUserRole':
          return createDataResponse(removeUserRoleAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'bulkAssignRole':
          return createDataResponse(bulkAssignRole(data.payload || data));

        case 'replaceUserRoles':
          return createDataResponse(replaceUserRoles(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateUserRoleAssignment':
          const assignmentUpdateData = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateUserRoleAssignment(assignmentUpdateData.assignment_id || assignmentUpdateData.id, assignmentUpdateData));

        case 'deleteUserRoleAssignment':
          const assignmentDeletion = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(deleteUserRoleAssignment(
            assignmentDeletion.assignment_id || assignmentDeletion.id,
            assignmentDeletion.reason || 'Admin deletion'
          ));

        case 'getExpiringAssignments':
          const expiringQuery = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(getExpiringAssignments(expiringQuery.days_ahead || 30));

        // v9.1 writes
        case 'generateTicketNumber':
          return createDataResponse(generateTicketNumberAction(data.company_id, data.ticket_type_id));

        case 'createWorkflowStep':
          return createDataResponse(createWorkflowStep(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateWorkflowStep':
          const stepUpdate = data.payload || data;
          return createDataResponse(updateWorkflowStep(stepUpdate.step_id || stepUpdate.id, stepUpdate));

        case 'deleteWorkflowStep':
          return createDataResponse(deleteWorkflowStep(data.step_id || data.id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'copyWorkflowSteps':
          return createDataResponse(copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany(data.ticket_type_id, data.source_company_id,
            data.target_company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'createDefaultWorkflow':
          return createDataResponse(createDefaultWorkflow(data.ticket_type_id, data.company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'updateCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(updateCustomField(data.field_id || data.id, data));

        case 'deleteCustomField':
          return createDataResponse(deleteCustomField(data.field_id || data.id, data.reason || 'Admin requested field deletion'));

        case 'createTicketLink':
          return createDataResponse(createTicketLinkAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'deleteTicketLink':
          return createDataResponse(deleteTicketLinkAction(data.link_id || data.id, data.reason));

        case 'lockCompany':
          return createDataResponse(lockCompany(data.id || data.company_id, data.reason, data.user_id || null));

        case 'unlockCompany':
          return createDataResponse(unlockCompany(data.id || data.company_id, data.reason, data.user_id || null));

        case 'saveCommentRequirements':
          return createDataResponse(saveCommentRequirements(data.payload || data));

        // v9.2 writes
        case 'saveStepConditions':
          return createDataResponse(saveStepConditions(data.payload || data));

        case 'saveReportConfig':
          return createDataResponse(saveReportConfig(data.payload || data));

        // v9.3 writes
        case 'assignDropdownToCompany':
          return createDataResponse(assignDropdownToCompany(data.dropdown_list_id, data.company_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'assignDropdownGlobally':
          return createDataResponse(assignDropdownGlobally(data.dropdown_list_id, data.user_id || null));

        case 'removeDropdownCompanyAssignment':
          return createDataResponse(removeDropdownCompanyAssignment(data.dropdown_list_id, data.company_id || '', data.user_id || null));

        case 'updateDropdownCompanyAssignments':
          return createDataResponse(updateDropdownCompanyAssignments(data.dropdown_list_id, data.assignments, data.user_id || null));

        case 'createCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(createCompanySLARule(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(updateCompanySLARule(data.rule_id || data.id, data.payload || data));

        case 'deleteCompanySLARule':
          return createDataResponse(deleteCompanySLARule(data.rule_id || data.id, data.user_id || null, data.reason));

        case 'exportConfiguration':
          return createDataResponse(exportConfiguration(data.payload || data));

        case 'createConfigurationBackup':
          return createDataResponse(createConfigurationBackup(data.payload || data));

        case 'importConfiguration':
          return createDataResponse(importConfiguration(data.payload || data));

        case 'setTicketTypeFeatures':
          return createDataResponse(setTicketTypeFeatures(data.payload || data));

        case 'createTag':
          return createDataResponse(createTagAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'updateTag':
          return createDataResponse(updateTagAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'deleteTag':
          return createDataResponse(deleteTagAction(data.tag_id || data.id, data.user_id || null, data.reason));

        case 'assignTags':
          return createDataResponse(assignTagsAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'removeTags':
          return createDataResponse(removeTagsAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'shareTicket':
          return createDataResponse(shareTicketAction(data.payload || data));

        case 'revokeTicketShare':
          return createDataResponse(revokeTicketShareAction(data.payload || data));

        default:
          return createJsonResponse({
            success: false,
            status: 'error',
            error: 'Unknown action: ' + action
          });
      }
}

/**
 * Handles OPTIONS requests for CORS preflight checks. This is essential for
 * allowing the frontend (e.g., on Vercel) to communicate with this API.
 * Google Apps Script requires proper CORS handling for cross-origin requests.
 * @param {object} e - The event object from the OPTIONS request.
 * @returns {ContentService.TextOutput} - An empty response with CORS support.
 */
function doOptions(e) {
  // Google Apps Script doesn't support setHeader chaining
  // For Web Apps, CORS is handled automatically when deployed with "Anyone" access
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput('')
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.TEXT);
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * CORE BUSINESS LOGIC FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Create a ticket: number it, write it by header, count it against the company (locking the company code on
 * the first ticket, spec 3.3), log it, start its workflow and save its custom field values.
 * v9.1: header-driven row with description / priority / created_by (the positional row dropped them);
 * customData falls back to custom_fields; the id is max(id) + 1 so a deleted row can't cause a duplicate.
 * @param {Object} payload - { title, description, priority, ticket_type_id, company_id, requester_id, customData }
 * @returns {Object} { id, ticketId, ticket_number, ticketNumber, workflow, customFieldErrors, message }
 */
function createTicket(payload) {
  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  ['title', 'ticket_type_id', 'company_id', 'requester_id'].forEach(field => {
    if (!payload[field]) throw new Error(`Missing required field: ${field}`);
  });

  const ticketsSheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const now = new Date();

  const ticketTypes = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);
  const companies = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const ticketType = (ticketTypes ? ticketTypes.records : []).find(t => sameId(t.id, payload.ticket_type_id));
  const company = (companies ? companies.records : []).find(c => sameId(c.id, payload.company_id));
  if (!ticketType) throw new Error(`Ticket Type with ID ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found.`);
  if (!company) throw new Error(`Company with ID ${payload.company_id} not found.`);

  const existing = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const ids = (existing ? existing.records : []).map(t => Number(t.id)).filter(id => isFinite(id));
  const newTicketId = (ids.length ? Math.max(...ids) : 0) + 1;

  const ticketNumber = generateTicketNumber(company.code, ticketType.code);

  ticketsSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(ticketsSheet), {
    id: newTicketId,
    ticket_number: ticketNumber,
    title: payload.title,
    ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
    requester_id: payload.requester_id,
    status: 'New',
    current_step_id: '',
    step_due_date: '',
    created_at: now,
    updated_at: now,
    company_id: payload.company_id,
    description: payload.description || '',
    priority: payload.priority || 'medium',
    is_active: true,
    created_by: payload.requester_id,
    updated_by: payload.requester_id
  }));

  // Spec 3.3: the company code locks on the first ticket, so existing ticket numbers stay valid
  const companyChanges = { ticket_count: (Number(company.ticket_count) || 0) + 1 };
  if (!isLockedFlag(company.code_locked)) {
    companyChanges.code_locked = true;
    companyChanges.code_locked_at = now.toISOString();
    companyChanges.code_locked_reason = 'first_ticket_created';
  }
  updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, company.id, companyChanges);

  logTicketHistory(newTicketId, payload.requester_id, 'Created', 'Ticket submitted by user.');
  logTicketAction(newTicketId, payload.requester_id, 'CREATE_TICKET', { newTicketData: payload });

  // v9.0: put the ticket on the first step of its workflow
  const workflow = startTicketWorkflow(newTicketId, payload.requester_id);

  // v8.3 (CR-01): header-driven via setCustomFieldValue
  const customData = payload.customData && Object.keys(payload.customData).length ? payload.customData : (payload.custom_fields || {});
  const customFieldErrors = [];
  Object.keys(customData).forEach(fieldId => {
    const result = setCustomFieldValue(newTicketId, fieldId, customData[fieldId], 'ticket');
    if (result.status === 'error') {
      customFieldErrors.push(`${fieldId}: ${result.message}`);
    }
  });

  return {
    id: newTicketId,
    ticketId: newTicketId,
    ticket_number: ticketNumber,
    ticketNumber: ticketNumber,
    workflow: workflow.data,
    customFieldErrors: customFieldErrors,
    message: customFieldErrors.length > 0
      ? "Ticket created, but some custom field values were not saved."
      : "Ticket created successfully."
  };
}

/**
 * Fetches all tickets with their links.
 * v9.2: only active links count; chained_children / chained_parent carry the links a step created
 * (link_type 'chained') for the dashboard's parent/child view. children / parent keep every active link.
 * @param {object} params - Optional filters ({ status }).
 * @returns {Array} - Ticket objects.
 */
function getTickets(params) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
  const tickets = getSheetDataAsJSON(ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS));
  const links = activeTicketLinks();

  const ticketsWithLinks = tickets.map(ticket => {
    const childLinks = links.filter(l => sameId(l.parent_ticket_id, ticket.id));
    const parentLinks = links.filter(l => sameId(l.child_ticket_id, ticket.id));
    const chainedParent = parentLinks.find(l => String(l.link_type) === 'chained');
    return {
      ...ticket,
      children: childLinks.map(l => l.child_ticket_id),
      parent: parentLinks.length ? parentLinks[0].parent_ticket_id : null,
      chained_children: childLinks.filter(l => String(l.link_type) === 'chained').map(l => l.child_ticket_id),
      chained_parent: chainedParent ? chainedParent.parent_ticket_id : null
    };
  });

  if (params && params.status) {
    return ticketsWithLinks.filter(t => t.status === params.status);
  }
  return ticketsWithLinks;
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * COMPANY MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

function getCompanies(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      initializeCompaniesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const companies = getSheetDataAsJSON(companiesSheet);
    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${companies.length} companies`);
    return companies;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting companies: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve companies: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

function getCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const companies = getCompanies();
    const company = companies.find(c => c.id === companyId);

    if (!company) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    return company;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting company ${companyId}: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function createCompany(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      companiesSheet = initializeCompaniesSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    // Validate required fields
    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Company name and code are required');
    }

    // Check if code already exists (among active companies only)
    const existingCompanies = getSheetDataAsJSON(companiesSheet);
    const activeCompanies = filterActiveRecords(existingCompanies);
    const codeExists = activeCompanies.some(c => c.code.toUpperCase() === payload.code.toUpperCase());

    if (codeExists) {
      throw new Error('Company code already exists');
    }

    // Create audit fields for new record
    const auditFields = createAuditFields();

    // Generate new company with comprehensive audit fields
    const newCompany = {
      id: generateId('comp'),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase(),
      code_locked: false,
      code_locked_at: null,
      code_locked_reason: null,
      ticket_count: 0,
      ...auditFields
    };

    // Add to sheet with all audit fields
    companiesSheet.appendRow([
      newCompany.id,
      newCompany.name,
      newCompany.code,
      newCompany.code_locked,
      newCompany.code_locked_at,
      newCompany.code_locked_reason,
      newCompany.ticket_count,
      newCompany.is_active,
      newCompany.created_at,
      newCompany.created_by,
      newCompany.updated_at,
      newCompany.updated_by,
      newCompany.deactivated_at,
      newCompany.deactivated_by,
      newCompany.deactivation_reason
    ]);

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('CREATE_COMPANY', newCompany.id, newCompany);

    Logger.log(`Created company: ${newCompany.name} (${newCompany.code})`);
    return newCompany;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Deactivate (soft delete) a company
 * @param {string} companyId - The company ID to deactivate
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deactivation
 * @returns {Object} Result of the deactivation operation
 */
function deactivateCompany(companyId, reason = 'admin_request') {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }

    // Find the row to deactivate
    const data = companiesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row => row[0] === companyId);
    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const actualRowIndex = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

    // Create deactivation audit fields
    const deactivationFields = deactivateAuditFields(reason);
    const sheetsFields = auditFieldsForSheets(deactivationFields);

    // Update the row with deactivation fields
    const currentRow = rows[rowIndex];
    const updatedRow = [...currentRow];

    // Update audit fields (assuming column positions match the schema)
    updatedRow[7] = sheetsFields.is_active; // is_active column
    updatedRow[10] = sheetsFields.updated_at; // updated_at column
    updatedRow[11] = sheetsFields.updated_by; // updated_by column
    updatedRow[12] = sheetsFields.deactivated_at; // deactivated_at column
    updatedRow[13] = sheetsFields.deactivated_by; // deactivated_by column
    updatedRow[14] = sheetsFields.deactivation_reason; // deactivation_reason column

    // Write the updated row back to the sheet
    companiesSheet.getRange(actualRowIndex, 1, 1, updatedRow.length).setValues([updatedRow]);

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('DEACTIVATE_COMPANY', companyId, { reason: reason });

    Logger.log(`Deactivated company: ${companyId} (Reason: ${reason})`);
    return {
      id: companyId,
      deactivated: true,
      reason: reason,
      deactivated_at: deactivationFields.deactivated_at
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deactivating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Reactivate a deactivated company
 * @param {string} companyId - The company ID to reactivate
 * @returns {Object} Result of the reactivation operation
 */
function reactivateCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    if (!companiesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }

    // Find the row to reactivate
    const data = companiesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);

    const rowIndex = rows.findIndex(row => row[0] === companyId);
    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const actualRowIndex = rowIndex + 2; // +1 for header, +1 for 0-based index

    // Create reactivation audit fields
    const reactivationFields = reactivateAuditFields();
    const sheetsFields = auditFieldsForSheets(reactivationFields);

    // Update the row with reactivation fields
    const currentRow = rows[rowIndex];
    const updatedRow = [...currentRow];

    // Update audit fields (assuming column positions match the schema)
    updatedRow[7] = sheetsFields.is_active; // is_active column
    updatedRow[10] = sheetsFields.updated_at; // updated_at column
    updatedRow[11] = sheetsFields.updated_by; // updated_by column
    updatedRow[12] = sheetsFields.deactivated_at; // deactivated_at column (cleared)
    updatedRow[13] = sheetsFields.deactivated_by; // deactivated_by column (cleared)
    updatedRow[14] = sheetsFields.deactivation_reason; // deactivation_reason column (cleared)

    // Write the updated row back to the sheet
    companiesSheet.getRange(actualRowIndex, 1, 1, updatedRow.length).setValues([updatedRow]);

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('REACTIVATE_COMPANY', companyId, { reactivated: true });

    Logger.log(`Reactivated company: ${companyId}`);
    return {
      id: companyId,
      reactivated: true,
      reactivated_at: reactivationFields.updated_at
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error reactivating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Update a company's name / code.
 * v9.1: the whole row is written by header (A1). Before, a positional 5-column write put created_at and
 * updated_at into code_locked / code_locked_at on every edit. A locked code can't change (spec 3.3) - unlock
 * it first (unlockCompany, admin override).
 * @param {Object} payload - { id, name, code, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} The updated company record
 */
function updateCompany(payload) {
  try {
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }
    if (!payload.id) {
      throw new Error('Company ID is required for update');
    }
    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Company name and code are required');
    }

    const table = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
    if (!table) {
      throw new Error('Companies sheet not found');
    }
    const current = table.records.find(c => sameId(c.id, payload.id));
    if (!current) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    const code = String(payload.code).toUpperCase().trim();
    const codeExists = table.records.some(c => !sameId(c.id, payload.id) && String(c.code || '').toUpperCase().trim() === code);
    if (codeExists) {
      throw new Error('Company code already exists');
    }
    if (code !== String(current.code || '').toUpperCase().trim() && isLockedFlag(current.code_locked)) {
      throw new Error('Company code is locked because tickets use it; unlock it first (admin override)');
    }

    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, payload.id, {
      name: String(payload.name).trim(),
      code: code,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: userId
    });

    logAdminAction(userId, 'UPDATE_COMPANY', 'company', payload.id, {
      before: { name: current.name, code: current.code },
      after: { name: result.after.name, code: result.after.code }
    });

    Logger.log(`Updated company: ${result.after.name}`);
    return result.after;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteCompany(companyId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const companiesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMPANIES);

    // Find the row to delete
    const data = companiesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let companyData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === companyId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1; // 1-based index
        companyData = data[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Company not found');
    }

    // Delete the row
    companiesSheet.deleteRow(rowIndex);

    // Log the action
    logAdminAction('DELETE_COMPANY', companyId, { deletedCompany: companyData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted company: ${companyData[1]}`);
    return { message: 'Company deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting company: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * ROLE MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get roles. v9.0: deactivated roles are hidden unless params.include_inactive; rows written before
 * roles had an is_active column (blank) count as active.
 * @param {Object} params - { companyId | company_id, include_inactive }
 * @returns {Array} Role records
 */
function getRoles(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ROLES);

    if (!rolesSheet) {
      rolesSheet = initializeRolesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    let roles = getSheetDataAsJSON(rolesSheet);
    const companyId = params.companyId || params.company_id;
    const includeInactive = String(params.include_inactive) === 'true';

    if (!includeInactive) {
      roles = roles.filter(r => isActiveFlag(r.is_active));
    }

    // Filter by company if specified (global roles have no company)
    if (companyId) {
      roles = roles.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId) || !r.company_id);
    }

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${roles.length} roles`);
    return roles;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting roles: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve roles: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Create a role. v9.0: header-driven (A1) and accepts role_type_id.
 * @param {Object} payload - { name, company_id ('global' or blank = global), role_type_id }
 * @returns {Object} The new role
 */
function createRole(payload) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
  const rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ROLES) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.ROLES);

  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  if (!payload.name || !String(payload.name).trim()) {
    throw new Error('Role name is required');
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const newRole = {
    id: generateId('role'),
    name: String(payload.name).trim(),
    company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? '' : (payload.company_id || ''),
    role_type_id: payload.role_type_id || '',
    is_active: true,
    created_at: now,
    created_by: getCurrentUserId(),
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: getCurrentUserId()
  };

  rolesSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(rolesSheet), newRole));
  logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_ROLE', 'role', newRole.id, { name: newRole.name, company_id: newRole.company_id });

  Logger.log(`Created role: ${newRole.name}`);
  return newRole;
}

/**
 * Update a role's name, company or role type. v9.0: reads the row and writes it back by header (A1).
 * @param {Object} payload - { id, name, company_id, role_type_id }
 * @returns {Object} The updated role
 */
function updateRole(payload) {
  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error('Payload is required');
  }
  if (!payload.id || !payload.name) {
    throw new Error('Role ID and name are required');
  }

  const changes = {
    name: String(payload.name).trim(),
    company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? '' : (payload.company_id || '')
  };
  if (payload.role_type_id !== undefined) changes.role_type_id = payload.role_type_id || '';
  if (payload.is_active !== undefined) changes.is_active = isActiveFlag(payload.is_active);

  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.ROLES, payload.id, changes);
  if (!result) {
    throw new Error('Role not found');
  }

  logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_ROLE', 'role', payload.id, changes);
  Logger.log(`Updated role: ${changes.name}`);
  return result.after;
}

/**
 * Deactivate a role (v9.0: soft delete; roles are never hard-deleted - A4).
 * Before v9.0 the deleteRole route called deactivateRole(), which never existed, so every delete failed.
 * @param {string} roleId - Role ID
 * @param {string} reason - Deactivation reason
 * @returns {Object} deactivateRecord() result ({ status, data, message })
 */
function deleteRole(roleId, reason = 'admin_request') {
  return deactivateRecord(SHEETS.ROLES, roleId, reason);
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DROPDOWN LIST MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Dropdown lists with their options and company access.
 * v9.3: each list carries assignments (live dropdown_company_assignments) and availability
 * ({ mode: global | companies | draft | legacy, company_ids }); with company_id only the lists that company
 * can use are returned (a draft list - assignments removed - is admin-only and never returned for a company).
 * @param {Object} params - { company_id } (optional)
 * @returns {Array} dropdown lists
 */
function getDropdownLists(params = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const lists = getSheetDataAsJSON(listsSheet);
    const assignmentTable = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_COMPANY_ASSIGNMENTS);
    const assignmentRows = assignmentTable ? assignmentTable.records : [];
    const companyId = (params && (params.company_id || params.companyId)) || null;

    const listsWithOptions = lists
      .map(list => ({
        ...list,
        options: getDropdownOptions(list.id),
        assignments: liveDropdownAssignments(list.id, assignmentRows.filter(liveRecord)),
        availability: dropdownAvailability(list, assignmentRows)
      }))
      .filter(list => !companyId || dropdownAvailableTo(list.availability, companyId));

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${listsWithOptions.length} dropdown lists`);
    return listsWithOptions;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting dropdown lists: ${error.message}`);
    throw new Error(`Failed to retrieve dropdown lists: ${error.message}`);
  }
}

function getDropdownOptions(listId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    if (!optionsSheet) {
      optionsSheet = initializeDropdownOptionsSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const allOptions = getSheetDataAsJSON(optionsSheet);
    const listOptions = allOptions.filter(opt => opt.dropdown_list_id === listId);

    return listOptions;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting dropdown options for list ${listId}: ${error.message}`);
    return [];
  }
}

function createDropdownList(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    let optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
    }
    if (!optionsSheet) {
      optionsSheet = initializeDropdownOptionsSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.name) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list name is required');
    }

    // Handle multi-company assignment
    let companyId = payload.company_id || null;

    // If multiple companies are specified, store as JSON array
    if (Array.isArray(payload.company_ids) && payload.company_ids.length > 0) {
      companyId = JSON.stringify(payload.company_ids);
    } else if (Array.isArray(payload.company_id) && payload.company_id.length > 0) {
      companyId = JSON.stringify(payload.company_id);
    }

    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();

    const newList = {
      id: generateId('dd'),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description ? payload.description.trim() : '',
      company_id: companyId,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: currentTime,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: currentTime,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: null,
      deactivated_by: null,
      deactivation_reason: null
    };

    // ENHANCED SHEET WRITING WITH VERIFICATION
    Logger.log('About to write to sheet - newList data:', JSON.stringify(newList, null, 2));
    Logger.log('Sheet name:', listsSheet.getName());
    Logger.log('Sheet ID:', listsSheet.getSheetId());

    const rowToAppend = [
      newList.id,
      newList.name,
      newList.description,
      newList.company_id,
      newList.is_active,
      newList.created_at,
      newList.created_by,
      newList.updated_at,
      newList.updated_by,
      newList.deactivated_at,
      newList.deactivated_by,
      newList.deactivation_reason
    ];

    Logger.log('Row data to append:', JSON.stringify(rowToAppend, null, 2));

    const beforeRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log('Row count before append:', beforeRowCount);

    // Write to sheet
    listsSheet.appendRow(rowToAppend);

    const afterRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log('Row count after append:', afterRowCount);
    Logger.log('Row count difference:', afterRowCount - beforeRowCount);

    // Verify the write by reading back the last row
    if (afterRowCount > beforeRowCount) {
      const lastRow = listsSheet.getRange(afterRowCount, 1, 1, 12).getValues()[0];
      Logger.log('Verification - last row written:', JSON.stringify(lastRow, null, 2));
    } else {
      Logger.error('ERROR: Row count did not increase after appendRow!');
    }

    // Add options if provided
    if (payload.options && Array.isArray(payload.options)) {
      Logger.log('Adding options:', payload.options.length);
      payload.options.forEach((option, index) => {
        if (option.label && option.value) {
          const optionRow = [
            generateId('opt'),
            newList.id,
            option.label.trim(),
            option.value.trim(),
            option.parent_option_id || ''
          ];
          Logger.log(`Adding option ${index + 1}:`, JSON.stringify(optionRow, null, 2));
          optionsSheet.appendRow(optionRow);
        }
      });
    }

    // Log admin action
    try {
      logAdminAction('CREATE_DROPDOWN_LIST', newList.id, newList);
      Logger.log('Admin action logged successfully');
    } catch (logError) {
      Logger.error('Error logging admin action:', logError.message);
    }

    Logger.log('=== SHEET OPERATIONS COMPLETE ===');
    Logger.log(`Created dropdown list: ${newList.name}`);
    return newList;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function updateDropdownList(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    const optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.id || !payload.name) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list ID and name are required');
    }

    // Update the list
    const listData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let listRowIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 1; i < listData.length; i++) {
      if (listData[i][0] === payload.id) {
        listRowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (listRowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list not found');
    }

    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = getCurrentUserId();

    const updatedList = {
      id: payload.id,
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description ? payload.description.trim() : (listData[listRowIndex - 1][2] || ''),
      company_id: payload.company_id !== undefined ? payload.company_id : listData[listRowIndex - 1][3],
      is_active: listData[listRowIndex - 1][4] !== undefined ? listData[listRowIndex - 1][4] : true,
      created_at: listData[listRowIndex - 1][5] || currentTime,
      created_by: listData[listRowIndex - 1][6] || currentUser,
      updated_at: currentTime,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: listData[listRowIndex - 1][9] || null,
      deactivated_by: listData[listRowIndex - 1][10] || null,
      deactivation_reason: listData[listRowIndex - 1][11] || null
    };

    listsSheet.getRange(listRowIndex, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[
      updatedList.id,
      updatedList.name,
      updatedList.description,
      updatedList.company_id,
      updatedList.is_active,
      updatedList.created_at,
      updatedList.created_by,
      updatedList.updated_at,
      updatedList.updated_by,
      updatedList.deactivated_at,
      updatedList.deactivated_by,
      updatedList.deactivation_reason
    ]]);

    // Delete existing options
    const optionsData = optionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = optionsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (optionsData[i][1] === payload.id) {
        optionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }

    // Add new options
    if (payload.options && Array.isArray(payload.options)) {
      payload.options.forEach(option => {
        if (option.label && option.value) {
          optionsSheet.appendRow([
            generateId('opt'),
            payload.id,
            option.label.trim(),
            option.value.trim(),
            option.parentValue || ''
          ]);
        }
      });
    }

    logAdminAction('UPDATE_DROPDOWN_LIST', payload.id, updatedList);

    Logger.log(`Updated dropdown list: ${updatedList.name}`);
    return updatedList;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteDropdownList(listId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);
    const optionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);

    // Find and delete the list
    const listData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let listRowIndex = -1;
    let deletedListData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < listData.length; i++) {
      if (listData[i][0] === listId) {
        listRowIndex = i + 1;
        deletedListData = listData[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (listRowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Dropdown list not found');
    }

    // Delete options first
    const optionsData = optionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = optionsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (optionsData[i][1] === listId) {
        optionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }

    // Delete the list
    listsSheet.deleteRow(listRowIndex);

    logAdminAction('DELETE_DROPDOWN_LIST', listId, { deletedList: deletedListData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted dropdown list: ${deletedListData[1]}`);
    return { message: 'Dropdown list deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting dropdown list: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * TICKET TYPE MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get ticket types, optionally for one company (its own types plus global ones with a blank company_id).
 * v9.1: accepts company_id or companyId and compares ids as strings; before, the filter kept only types
 * whose company_id was exactly null, which a sheet cell never is, so global types disappeared.
 * @param {Object} params - { company_id | companyId }
 * @returns {Array} Ticket type records
 */
function getTicketTypes(params = {}) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);
    if (!table) {
      initializeTicketTypesSheet();
      return [];
    }

    const companyId = params.company_id || params.companyId || null;
    const ticketTypes = table.records.filter(tt => !companyId || !tt.company_id || sameId(tt.company_id, companyId));

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${ticketTypes.length} ticket types`);
    return ticketTypes;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting ticket types: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function createTicketType(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    if (!ticketTypesSheet) {
      ticketTypesSheet = initializeTicketTypesSheet();
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type name and code are required');
    }

    const newTicketType = {
      id: generateId('tt'),
      transaction_id: payload.transaction_id || generateId('tr'),
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase().trim(),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description || '',
      is_active: payload.is_active !== false,
      require_attachment_on_create: payload.require_attachment_on_create || false,
      company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? null : payload.company_id,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    ticketTypesSheet.appendRow([
      newTicketType.id,
      newTicketType.transaction_id,
      newTicketType.code,
      newTicketType.name,
      newTicketType.description,
      newTicketType.is_active,
      newTicketType.require_attachment_on_create,
      newTicketType.company_id || '',
      newTicketType.created_at,
      newTicketType.updated_at
    ]);

    logAdminAction('CREATE_TICKET_TYPE', newTicketType.id, newTicketType);

    Logger.log(`Created ticket type: ${newTicketType.name}`);
    return newTicketType;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error creating ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function updateTicketType(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload) {
      throw new Error('Payload is required');
    }

    if (!payload.id || !payload.name || !payload.code) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type ID, name, and code are required');
    }

    // Find and update the ticket type
    const data = ticketTypesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === payload.id) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type not found');
    }

    const updatedTicketType = {
      id: payload.id,
      transaction_id: payload.transaction_id || data[rowIndex - 1][1],
      code: payload.code.toUpperCase().trim(),
      name: payload.name.trim(),
      description: payload.description || '',
      is_active: payload.is_active !== false,
      require_attachment_on_create: payload.require_attachment_on_create || false,
      company_id: payload.company_id === 'global' ? null : payload.company_id,
      created_at: data[rowIndex - 1][8],
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    ticketTypesSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, 10).setValues([[
      updatedTicketType.id,
      updatedTicketType.transaction_id,
      updatedTicketType.code,
      updatedTicketType.name,
      updatedTicketType.description,
      updatedTicketType.is_active,
      updatedTicketType.require_attachment_on_create,
      updatedTicketType.company_id || '',
      updatedTicketType.created_at,
      updatedTicketType.updated_at
    ]]);

    logAdminAction('UPDATE_TICKET_TYPE', payload.id, updatedTicketType);

    Logger.log(`Updated ticket type: ${updatedTicketType.name}`);
    return updatedTicketType;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function deleteTicketType(ticketTypeId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const ticketTypesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES);

    const data = ticketTypesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let ticketTypeData = null;

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === ticketTypeId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        ticketTypeData = data[i];
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('Ticket type not found');
    }

    ticketTypesSheet.deleteRow(rowIndex);
    logAdminAction('DELETE_TICKET_TYPE', ticketTypeId, { deletedTicketType: ticketTypeData });

    Logger.log(`Deleted ticket type: ${ticketTypeData[3]}`);
    return { message: 'Ticket type deleted successfully' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error deleting ticket type: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * USER PREFERENCES FUNCTIONS
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Retrieves user preferences by user ID.
 * @param {string} userId - The user's ID.
 * @returns {object} - The user's preferences or default preferences.
 */
function getUserPreferences(userId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let preferencesSheet;

    try {
      preferencesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES);
    } catch (error) {
      // If sheet doesn't exist, initialize it
      preferencesSheet = initializeUserPreferencesSheet();
    }

    const data = preferencesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();

    // Look for existing user preferences
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === userId) {
        // Found existing preferences, return them
        return {
          user_id: data[i][0],
          dark_mode: data[i][1] || false,
          timezone: data[i][2] || 'Asia/Manila',
          language: data[i][3] || 'en',
          email_notifications: data[i][4] !== false, // Default to true
          desktop_notifications: data[i][5] !== false, // Default to true
          dashboard_layout: data[i][6] || 'grid',
          items_per_page: data[i][7] || 20,
          auto_refresh: data[i][8] !== false, // Default to true
          refresh_interval: data[i][9] || 30000,
          created_at: data[i][10],
          updated_at: data[i][11]
        };
      }
    }

    // If no preferences found, return default preferences
    const defaultPreferences = {
      user_id: userId,
      dark_mode: false,
      timezone: 'Asia/Manila',
      language: 'en',
      email_notifications: true,
      desktop_notifications: true,
      dashboard_layout: 'grid',
      items_per_page: 20,
      auto_refresh: true,
      refresh_interval: 30000,
      created_at: null,
      updated_at: null
    };

    Logger.log(`No existing preferences found for user ${userId}, returning defaults`);
    return defaultPreferences;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error getting user preferences: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * Updates or creates user preferences.
 * @param {string} userId - The user's ID.
 * @param {object} preferences - The preferences to update.
 * @returns {object} - The updated preferences.
 */
function updateUserPreferences(userId, preferences) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let preferencesSheet;

    try {
      preferencesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES);
    } catch (error) {
      // If sheet doesn't exist, initialize it
      preferencesSheet = initializeUserPreferencesSheet();
    }

    const data = preferencesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let existingPrefs = null;

    // Look for existing user preferences
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === userId) {
        rowIndex = i + 1; // Sheet rows are 1-indexed
        existingPrefs = {
          dark_mode: data[i][1] || false,
          timezone: data[i][2] || 'Asia/Manila',
          language: data[i][3] || 'en',
          email_notifications: data[i][4] !== false,
          desktop_notifications: data[i][5] !== false,
          dashboard_layout: data[i][6] || 'grid',
          items_per_page: data[i][7] || 20,
          auto_refresh: data[i][8] !== false,
          refresh_interval: data[i][9] || 30000,
          created_at: data[i][10],
          updated_at: data[i][11]
        };
        break;
      }
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const updatedPrefs = {
      user_id: userId,
      dark_mode: preferences.dark_mode !== undefined ? preferences.dark_mode : (existingPrefs?.dark_mode || false),
      timezone: preferences.timezone || existingPrefs?.timezone || 'Asia/Manila',
      language: preferences.language || existingPrefs?.language || 'en',
      email_notifications: preferences.email_notifications !== undefined ? preferences.email_notifications : (existingPrefs?.email_notifications !== false),
      desktop_notifications: preferences.desktop_notifications !== undefined ? preferences.desktop_notifications : (existingPrefs?.desktop_notifications !== false),
      dashboard_layout: preferences.dashboard_layout || existingPrefs?.dashboard_layout || 'grid',
      items_per_page: preferences.items_per_page || existingPrefs?.items_per_page || 20,
      auto_refresh: preferences.auto_refresh !== undefined ? preferences.auto_refresh : (existingPrefs?.auto_refresh !== false),
      refresh_interval: preferences.refresh_interval || existingPrefs?.refresh_interval || 30000,
      created_at: existingPrefs?.created_at || now,
      updated_at: now
    };

    const rowData = [
      updatedPrefs.user_id,
      updatedPrefs.dark_mode,
      updatedPrefs.timezone,
      updatedPrefs.language,
      updatedPrefs.email_notifications,
      updatedPrefs.desktop_notifications,
      updatedPrefs.dashboard_layout,
      updatedPrefs.items_per_page,
      updatedPrefs.auto_refresh,
      updatedPrefs.refresh_interval,
      updatedPrefs.created_at,
      updatedPrefs.updated_at
    ];

    if (rowIndex > -1) {
      // Update existing row
      preferencesSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, rowData.length).setValues([rowData]);
      Logger.log(`Updated preferences for user ${userId}`);
    } else {
      // Create new row
      preferencesSheet.appendRow(rowData);
      Logger.log(`Created new preferences for user ${userId}`);
    }

    // Log the admin action
    logAdminAction('UPDATE_USER_PREFERENCES', userId, {
      updatedFields: Object.keys(preferences),
      preferences: updatedPrefs
    });

    return updatedPrefs;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error updating user preferences: ${error.message}`);
    throw error;
  }
}

function initializeUserPreferencesSheet() {
  // v8.3 (CR-04/CR-05): headers from TABLE_SCHEMAS; never clears a tab that already has data
  return ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.USER_PREFERENCES);
}

/**
