/**
 * Workflow steps for a ticket type / company, in sort order.
 * v9.1: each step carries its active SLA (sla_duration, sla_unit, exclude_weekends) so the builder's edit
 * form can show it.
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type filter (optional)
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: steps, message }
 */
function getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId = null, companyId = null) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const slaTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
    const slaByStep = {};
    (slaTable ? slaTable.records : [])
      .filter(sla => isActiveFlag(sla.is_active) && !sla.deleted_at)
      .forEach(sla => { slaByStep[String(sla.step_id)] = sla; });

    const steps = table.records
      .filter(step => isActiveFlag(step.is_active) && !step.deleted_at && !step.deactivated_at)
      .filter(step => !ticketTypeId || sameId(step.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId))
      .filter(step => !companyId || sameId(step.company_id, companyId))
      .sort((a, b) => (Number(a.sort_order) || 0) - (Number(b.sort_order) || 0))
      .map(step => {
        const sla = slaByStep[String(step.id)];
        return {
          ...step,
          sla_duration: sla ? sla.duration : '',
          sla_unit: sla ? sla.unit : '',
          exclude_weekends: sla ? boolFlag(sla.exclude_weekends) : false
        };
      });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: steps,
      message: `Retrieved ${steps.length} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a workflow step (and its SLA when sla_duration is given).
 * v9.1: header-driven row, generateId (copying steps in one call collided on Date.now()), company_id
 * required (workflows are per company), SLA validated before anything is written.
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_type_id, company_id, name, status_on_reach, step_type, approver_logic,
 *   sort_order, next_ticket_type_id, external_app_url, completion_action_name, sla_duration, sla_unit,
 *   exclude_weekends, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: step, message }
 */
function createWorkflowStep(payload) {
  try {
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_type_id', 'company_id', 'name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const approverLogic = String(payload.approver_logic || 'any').toLowerCase();
    if (!['any', 'all'].includes(approverLogic)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'approver_logic must be "any" or "all"' };
    }

    const hasSLA = payload.sla_duration !== undefined && payload.sla_duration !== null && payload.sla_duration !== '';
    if (hasSLA) {
      const slaError = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
      if (slaError) return { status: 'error', message: slaError };
    }

    const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const now = new Date();

    const stepData = {
      id: generateId('ws'),
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      status_on_reach: payload.status_on_reach,
      step_type: payload.step_type,
      approver_logic: approverLogic,
      sort_order: payload.sort_order || 1,
      next_ticket_type_id: payload.next_ticket_type_id || '',
      external_app_url: payload.external_app_url || '',
      completion_action_name: payload.completion_action_name || '',
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: userId,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: userId
    };
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), stepData));

    if (hasSLA) {
      const sla = upsertStepSLA(stepData.id, payload, userId);
      if (sla.status !== 'success') return sla;
    }

    logAdminAction(userId, 'CREATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepData.id, {
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      step_type: payload.step_type,
      sla_duration: hasSLA ? payload.sla_duration : null
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { ...stepData, sla_duration: hasSLA ? payload.sla_duration : '', sla_unit: hasSLA ? payload.sla_unit : '' },
      message: 'Workflow step created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a workflow step and, when sla_duration is in the payload, its SLA (blank = remove the SLA).
 * v9.1: whole row written by header (updateRecordFields); ids compared as strings. A step can't move to
 * another ticket type or company.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Fields to change + sla_duration / sla_unit / exclude_weekends + user_id
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: step, message }
 */
function updateWorkflowStep(stepId, payload) {
  try {
    if (!stepId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
    }
    const found = findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId);
    if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    const changes = pickDefined(payload, ['name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type', 'approver_logic', 'sort_order',
      'next_ticket_type_id', 'external_app_url', 'completion_action_name']);
    if (changes.approver_logic !== undefined) {
      changes.approver_logic = String(changes.approver_logic).toLowerCase();
      if (!['any', 'all'].includes(changes.approver_logic)) {
        return { status: 'error', message: 'approver_logic must be "any" or "all"' };
      }
    }
    ['next_ticket_type_id', 'external_app_url', 'completion_action_name'].forEach(key => {
      if (changes[key] === null) changes[key] = '';
    });

    const slaGiven = payload.sla_duration !== undefined;
    const removeSLA = slaGiven && (payload.sla_duration === null || payload.sla_duration === '');
    if (slaGiven && !removeSLA) {
      const slaError = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
      if (slaError) return { status: 'error', message: slaError };
    }

    const userId = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId, {
      ...changes,
      updated_at: new Date(),
      updated_by: userId
    });

    if (removeSLA) {
      removeStepSLA(stepId, userId, 'SLA removed in workflow builder');
    } else if (slaGiven) {
      const sla = upsertStepSLA(stepId, payload, userId);
      if (sla.status !== 'success') return sla;
    }

    logAdminAction(userId, 'UPDATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepId, {
      ...changes,
      sla_duration: slaGiven ? payload.sla_duration : undefined
    });

    return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'Workflow step updated successfully' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete (deactivate) a workflow step and its SLA.
 * v9.1: refused while an open ticket sits on the step (it would have no step to approve).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {string} userId - Admin (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id, deactivated }, message }
 */
function deleteWorkflowStep(stepId, userId = null) {
  try {
    if (!stepId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
    }
    const found = findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId);
    if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    const tickets = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
    const openTickets = (tickets ? tickets.records : []).filter(t =>
      sameId(t.current_step_id, stepId) && isActiveFlag(t.is_active) && !t.cancelled_at &&
      !TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status));
    if (openTickets.length > 0) {
      return {
        status: 'error',
        message: `${openTickets.length} open ticket(s) are at this step (${openTickets.slice(0, 5).map(t => t.ticket_number || t.id).join(', ')}) - move or close them first`
      };
    }

    const result = deactivateRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId, 'Admin deletion');
    if (result.status !== 'success') return result;
    removeStepSLA(stepId, userId || getCurrentUserId(), 'Workflow step deleted');

    return { status: 'success', data: { id: stepId, deactivated: true }, message: 'Workflow step deleted successfully' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Reorder workflow steps for a ticket type
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
 * @param {Array} stepIds - Array of step IDs in desired order
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming reorder
 */
function reorderWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, stepIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(stepIds) || stepIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');
    const ticketTypeIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_type_id');
    const sortOrderIndex = headers.indexOf('sort_order');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (idIndex === -1 || ticketTypeIndex === -1 || sortOrderIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Required columns not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();
    let updatedCount = 0;

    // Update sort order for each step
    stepIds.forEach((stepId, index) => {
      const newSortOrder = index + 1;

      for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (data[i][idIndex] === stepId && data[i][ticketTypeIndex] === ticketTypeId) {
          sheet.getRange(i + 1, sortOrderIndex + 1).setValue(newSortOrder);
          if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
          if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
          updatedCount++;
          break;
        }
      }
    });

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'REORDER_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', ticketTypeId, {
      step_ids: stepIds,
      updated_count: updatedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId, updated_count: updatedCount },
      message: `Reordered ${updatedCount} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reorderWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Clone workflow steps from one ticket type to another
 * @param {string} fromTicketType - Source ticket type ID
 * @param {string} toTicketType - Target ticket type ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with cloned steps data
 */
function cloneWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType, toTicketType) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get source steps
    const sourceSteps = getWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType);
    if (sourceSteps.status !== 'success' || !sourceSteps.data.length) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps found to clone' };
    }

    const clonedSteps = [];
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Clone each step
    for (const sourceStep of sourceSteps.data) {
      const newStepId = 'ws_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);

      const clonedStep = {
        id: newStepId,
        ticket_type_id: toTicketType,
        company_id: sourceStep.company_id,
        name: sourceStep.name,
        status_on_reach: sourceStep.status_on_reach,
        step_type: sourceStep.step_type,
        approver_logic: sourceStep.approver_logic,
        sort_order: sourceStep.sort_order,
        next_ticket_type_id: sourceStep.next_ticket_type_id,
        external_app_url: sourceStep.external_app_url,
        completion_action_name: sourceStep.completion_action_name,
        is_active: true,
        created_at: now,
        created_by: currentUser,
        updated_at: now,
        updated_by: currentUser,
        deactivated_at: null,
        deactivated_by: null,
        deactivation_reason: null
      };

      // Add to sheet
      const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      const newRow = [];

      headers.forEach(header => {
        newRow.push(clonedStep[header] || '');
      });

      sheet.appendRow(newRow);
      clonedSteps.push(clonedStep);

      // Small delay to ensure unique timestamps
      Utilities.sleep(10);
    }

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CLONE_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', toTicketType, {
      from_ticket_type: fromTicketType,
      cloned_count: clonedSteps.length,
      step_ids: clonedSteps.map(step => step.id)
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: clonedSteps,
      message: `Cloned ${clonedSteps.length} workflow steps successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in cloneWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get the roles assigned as approvers of a workflow step.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ step_id, role_id }], message }
 */
function getStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    const approvers = table.records.filter(row => sameId(row.step_id, stepId));
    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: approvers,
      message: `Retrieved ${approvers.length} step approvers`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Assign roles as approvers to a workflow step (skips roles already assigned).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Role IDs to add
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, assigned_count }, message }
 */
function assignStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const existing = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    const existingRoleIds = (existing.data || []).map(approver => String(approver.role_id));
    const headers = sheetHeaders(sheet);
    let assignedCount = 0;

    roleIds.forEach(roleId => {
      if (!existingRoleIds.includes(String(roleId))) {
        sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(headers, { step_id: stepId, role_id: roleId }));
        existingRoleIds.push(String(roleId));
        assignedCount++;
      }
    });

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'ASSIGN_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      assigned_count: assignedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, assigned_count: assignedCount },
      message: `Assigned ${assignedCount} new approvers to step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in assignStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Remove role assignments from a workflow step.
 * step_approvers is a link table (step_id, role_id) with no status column, so the link rows are removed;
 * the admin action log records which roles were removed.
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Role IDs to remove
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, removed_count }, message }
 */
function removeStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const removeIds = roleIds.map(String);
    let removedCount = 0;

    // Bottom-up so the remaining row numbers stay valid
    for (let i = table.records.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const row = table.records[i];
      if (sameId(row.step_id, stepId) && removeIds.includes(String(row.role_id))) {
        table.sheet.deleteRow(table.rowNumbers[i]);
        removedCount++;
      }
    }

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'REMOVE_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      removed_count: removedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, removed_count: removedCount },
      message: `Removed ${removedCount} approvers from step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in removeStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate step approver assignments
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const approvers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (approvers.status !== 'success') {
      return approvers;
    }

    const issues = [];

    if (approvers.data.length === 0) {
      issues.push('No approvers assigned to step');
    }

    // Check if all assigned roles exist and are active
    // TODO: Add role existence validation

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        step_id: stepId,
        approver_count: approvers.data.length,
        issues: issues,
        is_valid: issues.length === 0
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Step approvers are valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get a user's role assignments. v9.0: date-aware (isAssignmentActive) unless include_inactive.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company filter (optional)
 * @param {Object} options - { include_inactive: true } returns every row, including expired/deleted
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignments[], message }
 */
function getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId = null, options = {}) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const assignments = table.records
      .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId))
      .filter(a => !companyId || sameId(a.company_id, companyId))
      .filter(a => options.include_inactive || isAssignmentActive(a, now));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignments,
      message: `Retrieved ${assignments.length} role assignments`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUserRoleAssignments: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a user role assignment, with the date-based fields (v9.0: header-driven, A1).
 * @param {Object} payload - user_id, role_id, company_id (required); ticket_type_id (blank = every type),
 *   assignment_type, effective_start_date, effective_end_date, validity_end_date, auto_expire_days,
 *   approval_required, assignment_notes, assigned_by_user_id
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: assignment, message }
 */
function createUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);

    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // v9.1 (review R-10): a company-specific role can only be assigned in its own company
    const role = findRecord(SHEETS.ROLES, payload.role_id);
    if (!role || !isActiveFlag(role.record.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Role ${payload.role_id} not found` };
    }
    if (role.record.company_id && !sameId(role.record.company_id, payload.company_id)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Role "${role.record.name}" belongs to another company` };
    }

    // An end date covers that whole day (endOfTerm); dates are stored as sent so a date-only value stays one
    const start = payload.effective_start_date ? toDate(payload.effective_start_date) : new Date();
    const end = payload.effective_end_date ? endOfTerm(payload.effective_end_date) : null;
    if (!start || (payload.effective_end_date && !end)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid effective_start_date / effective_end_date' };
    }
    if (end && end <= start) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'effective_end_date must be on or after effective_start_date' };
    }

    // Same role + ticket type + company that is current or not yet started counts as a duplicate
    const existing = getUserRoleAssignments(payload.user_id, payload.company_id, { include_inactive: true });
    const now = new Date();
    const duplicate = (existing.data || []).find(a =>
      sameId(a.role_id, payload.role_id) &&
      sameId(a.ticket_type_id || '', payload.ticket_type_id || '') &&
      isActiveFlag(a.is_active) && !a.deleted_at &&
      !(assignmentExpiry(a) && assignmentExpiry(a) <= now)
    );
    if (duplicate) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User already has this role assignment' };
    }

    const assignment = {
      id: generateId('ura'),
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id || '',
      role_id: payload.role_id,
      validity_end_date: payload.validity_end_date || '',
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      assignment_type: payload.assignment_type || 'permanent',
      effective_start_date: payload.effective_start_date || start.toISOString(),
      effective_end_date: payload.effective_end_date || '',
      auto_expire_days: payload.auto_expire_days || '',
      assigned_by_user_id: payload.assigned_by_user_id || getCurrentUserId(),
      approval_required: payload.approval_required === true || String(payload.approval_required) === 'true',
      assignment_notes: payload.assignment_notes || '',
      ...createAuditFields(payload.assigned_by_user_id)
    };

    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), assignment));

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignment.id, {
      user_id: assignment.user_id,
      role_id: assignment.role_id,
      company_id: assignment.company_id,
      assignment_type: assignment.assignment_type,
      effective_start_date: assignment.effective_start_date,
      effective_end_date: assignment.effective_end_date
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignment,
      message: 'User role assignment created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a user role assignment (v9.0: dates, type, notes and is_active; whole row written by header).
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Fields to change
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id, changes }, message }
 */
function updateUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId, payload) {
  try {
    const allowedFields = ['validity_end_date', 'effective_start_date', 'effective_end_date', 'auto_expire_days',
      'assignment_type', 'assignment_notes', 'approval_required', 'ticket_type_id', 'is_active'];
    const updates = {};
    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field) && payload[field] !== undefined) {
        updates[field] = field === 'is_active' ? isActiveFlag(payload[field]) : payload[field];
      }
    });

    const current = findRecord(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId);
    if (!current) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }

    const merged = { ...current.record, ...updates };
    const start = toDate(merged.effective_start_date);
    const end = merged.effective_end_date ? endOfTerm(merged.effective_end_date) : null;
    if (start && end && end <= start) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'effective_end_date must be on or after effective_start_date' };
    }

    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId, { ...updates, ...updateAuditFields() });
    const changes = {};
    Object.keys(updates).forEach(field => {
      if (String(result.before[field]) !== String(updates[field])) {
        changes[field] = { from: result.before[field], to: updates[field] };
      }
    });

    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'UPDATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId, changes: changes },
      message: 'User role assignment updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Soft-delete a user role assignment (deleted_* columns, is_active false).
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID
 * @param {string} reason - Deletion reason
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id }, message }
 */
function deleteUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId, reason = 'Admin deletion') {
  try {
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS, assignmentId, deleteAuditFields(reason));
    if (!result) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }

    logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'DELETE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, {
      reason: reason,
      user_id: result.before.user_id,
      role_id: result.before.role_id
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId },
      message: 'User role assignment deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get the users who can approve a workflow step.
 * v9.0: only assignments that are active today (isAssignmentActive), and - when a ticket is given - only
 * assignments for the ticket's company and ticket type (a blank ticket_type_id covers every type).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record (optional; without it, every company/type matches)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [{ user_id, role_id, company_id, ticket_type_id, ... }], message }
 */
function getUsersForStep(stepId, ticket = null) {
  try {
    const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (stepApprovers.status !== 'success') {
      return stepApprovers;
    }

    const roleIds = stepApprovers.data.map(approver => String(approver.role_id));
    if (roleIds.length === 0) {
      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: [],
        message: 'No roles assigned to this step'
      };
    }

    const table = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const eligibleUsers = table.records
      .filter(a => roleIds.includes(String(a.role_id)) && isAssignmentActive(a, now))
      .filter(a => !ticket || sameId(a.company_id, ticket.company_id))
      .filter(a => !ticket || !a.ticket_type_id || sameId(a.ticket_type_id, ticket.ticket_type_id))
      .map(a => ({
        user_id: a.user_id,
        role_id: a.role_id,
        company_id: a.company_id,
        ticket_type_id: a.ticket_type_id,
        effective_start_date: a.effective_start_date,
        effective_end_date: a.effective_end_date,
        validity_end_date: a.validity_end_date
      }));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: eligibleUsers,
      message: `Found ${eligibleUsers.length} users eligible for this step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUsersForStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate user role assignment data
 * @param {Object} payload - Assignment data to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const issues = [];

    // Check required fields
    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    requiredFields.forEach(field => {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        issues.push(`Missing required field: ${field}`);
      }
    });

    // Check validity end date format
    if (payload.validity_end_date) {
      if (!isValidDate(payload.validity_end_date)) {
        issues.push('Invalid validity_end_date format');
      } else if (new Date(payload.validity_end_date) <= new Date()) {
        issues.push('Validity end date must be in the future');
      }
    }

    // TODO: Add checks for user existence, role existence, company existence

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        is_valid: issues.length === 0,
        issues: issues
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Assignment data is valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get SLA configuration for a workflow step (v9.0: id compared as a string, A8).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: slas[], message }
 */
function getStepSLAs(stepId) {
  try {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const slas = table.records.filter(sla => isActiveFlag(sla.is_active) && !sla.deleted_at && sameId(sla.step_id, stepId));
    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slas,
      message: `Retrieved ${slas.length} SLA configurations`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepSLAs: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {Object} payload - SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created SLA data
 */
function createStepSLA(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['step_id', 'duration', 'unit'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Validate unit
    const validUnits = ['hours', 'days'];
    if (!validUnits.includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    // Validate duration
    if (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Check for existing SLA
    const existing = getStepSLAs(payload.step_id);
    if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'SLA already exists for this step. Use update function instead.' };
    }

    const slaId = 'sla_' + Date.now();
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const slaData = {
      id: slaId,
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit,
      exclude_weekends: payload.exclude_weekends || false,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deleted_at: null,
      deleted_by: null,
      deletion_reason: null
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(slaData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slaData,
      message: 'Step SLA created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated SLA data
 */
function updateStepSLA(slaId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Validate fields if provided
    if (payload.unit && !['hours', 'days'].includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    if (payload.duration && (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['duration', 'unit', 'exclude_weekends'];
    const changes = {};

    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);

    // Log admin action
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(currentUser, 'UPDATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Step SLA updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to delete
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming deletion
 */
function deleteStepSLA(slaId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    // Soft delete
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const deletedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_at');
    const deletedByIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_by');
    const deletionReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('deletion_reason');
    const isActiveIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    if (deletedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (deletedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    if (deletionReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletionReasonIndex + 1).setValue('Admin deletion');
    if (isActiveIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, isActiveIndex + 1).setValue(false);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'DELETE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      reason: 'Admin deletion',
      deleted_at: now
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId },
      message: 'Step SLA deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

// PHASE 1C: TICKET MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
// Purpose: Complete ticket lifecycle management with workflow progression
// Functions: 15 functions for ticket operations, history tracking, and workflow logic

/**
 * Create a ticket and start its workflow (used for chained tickets).
 * v9.0: delegates to createTicket(), which numbers the ticket and starts the workflow. The old body
 * called generateTicketNumber(typeId, companyId) and getTicketTypes(typeId, companyId) with the wrong
 * arguments, so every chained ticket failed.
 * @param {Object} payload - title, ticket_type_id, requester_id, company_id (+ description, priority,
 *   customData / custom_field_values)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: ticket, message }
 */
function createTicketWithWorkflow(payload) {
  try {
    const requiredFields = ['title', 'ticket_type_id', 'requester_id', 'company_id'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const created = createTicket({ ...payload, customData: payload.customData || payload.custom_field_values });
    const ticket = getTicketById(created.ticketId);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: ticket.status === 'success' ? ticket.data : { id: created.ticketId, ticket_number: created.ticketNumber },
      message: created.workflow && created.workflow.started
        ? 'Ticket created successfully and workflow started'
        : 'Ticket created (no workflow steps for this ticket type and company)'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketWithWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a ticket's title / description / priority (v9.0: whole row written by header).
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {Object} payload - { title, description, priority, comment, updated_by, custom_field_values }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { id, changes }, message }
 */
function updateTicketWithValidation(ticketId, payload) {
  try {
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    if (!isActiveFlag(ticket.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot update inactive ticket' };
    }
    if (ticket.cancelled_at) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot update cancelled ticket' };
    }

    const currentUser = payload.updated_by || payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
    const updates = pickDefined(payload, ['title', 'description', 'priority']);
    const changes = {};
    Object.keys(updates).forEach(field => {
      if (String(ticket[field]) !== String(updates[field])) {
        changes[field] = { from: ticket[field], to: updates[field] };
      }
    });

    updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId, { ...updates, updated_at: new Date(), updated_by: currentUser });

    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      createTicketHistoryEntry({
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        user_id: currentUser,
        action: 'UPDATED',
        comment: payload.comment || 'Ticket details updated',
        field_changes: changes
      });
      createTicketActionLog({
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        user_id: currentUser,
        action_type: 'TICKET_UPDATED',
        details: changes
      });
    }

    if (payload.custom_field_values && Object.keys(payload.custom_field_values).length > 0) {
      const customFieldResult = bulkUpdateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, payload.custom_field_values);
      if (customFieldResult.status !== 'success') {
        Logger.log('Warning: Custom field update failed: ' + customFieldResult.message);
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: ticketId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Ticket updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateTicketWithValidation: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Move a ticket through its workflow: approve, reject, return or complete.
 * v9.0:
 * - eligibility comes from checkUserCanApproveStep (ticket company + type, date-aware assignments)
 * - approver_logic 'all': an approval that doesn't yet cover every approver role is recorded as
 *   PARTIAL_APPROVAL (ticket stays on the step); the approval that completes the set advances it
 * - payload.step_id (optional) must match the ticket's current step, so a stale screen can't act
 * - processStepCompletion (chained tickets etc.) runs whenever the step is completed, last step included
 * - the ticket row is written by header (A1)
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} action - approve | reject | return | complete
 * @param {Object} payload - { user_id, comment, step_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_id, action_performed, new_status, next_step, step_due_date,
 *   partial, approved_role_ids, required_role_ids }, message }
 */
function progressTicketWorkflow(ticketId, action, payload) {
  try {
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    const userId = payload.user_id;
    const decision = String(action || '').toLowerCase();

    if (!userId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
    }
    if (!isActiveFlag(ticket.is_active) || ticket.cancelled_at || TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(ticket.status)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Cannot progress a ${ticket.cancelled_at ? 'cancelled' : 'closed or inactive'} ticket` };
    }
    if (!ticket.current_step_id) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket has no current workflow step' };
    }
    if (payload.step_id && !sameId(payload.step_id, ticket.current_step_id)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'The ticket has moved to another step - refresh and try again' };
    }

    const permission = checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticketId, ticket);
    if (permission.status !== 'success') {
      return permission;
    }
    // v9.1 (review R-03): approve needs can_approve; reject / return only need an approver role (can_act),
    // so a partial approver under 'all' can still stop the ticket
    const isApproval = decision === 'approve' || decision === 'complete';
    if (!(isApproval ? permission.data.can_approve : permission.data.can_act)) {
      return {
        status: 'error',
        message: isApproval && permission.data.already_approved
          ? 'You have already approved this step'
          : 'User does not have permission to perform this action on current step'
      };
    }

    // v9.1: comment requirements of the ticket type / company (spec 3.3)
    const missingComment = requireCommentFor(ticket, decision, payload.comment);
    if (missingComment) {
      return { status: 'error', message: missingComment };
    }

    const stepResult = getWorkflowStepById(ticket.current_step_id);
    const currentStep = stepResult.status === 'success' ? stepResult.data : { id: ticket.current_step_id };

    let nextStep = null;
    let newStatus = null;
    let actionType = '';
    let stepCompleted = false;

    switch (decision) {
      case 'approve': {
        const requiredRoles = permission.data.required_role_ids;
        const approvedRoles = unique(permission.data.approved_role_ids.concat(permission.data.eligible_role_ids));
        const needsAll = String(permission.data.approver_logic).toLowerCase() === 'all';

        if (needsAll && !requiredRoles.every(roleId => approvedRoles.includes(roleId))) {
          createTicketHistoryEntry({
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            user_id: userId,
            action: 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL',
            comment: payload.comment || 'Approved (waiting for other approver roles)',
            from_status: ticket.status,
            to_status: ticket.status,
            from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
            to_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
            field_changes: { role_ids: permission.data.eligible_role_ids }
          });
          createTicketActionLog({
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            user_id: userId,
            action_type: 'WORKFLOW_PARTIAL_APPROVAL',
            details: { step: ticket.current_step_id, approved_role_ids: approvedRoles, required_role_ids: requiredRoles }
          });
          return {
            status: 'success',
            data: {
              ticket_id: ticketId,
              action_performed: 'approve',
              new_status: ticket.status,
              next_step: currentStep,
              step_due_date: ticket.step_due_date,
              partial: true,
              approved_role_ids: approvedRoles,
              required_role_ids: requiredRoles
            },
            message: `Approval recorded (${approvedRoles.filter(r => requiredRoles.includes(r)).length} of ${requiredRoles.length} approver roles)`
          };
        }

        const nextStepResult = calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, 'APPROVE');
        if (nextStepResult.status !== 'success') {
          return nextStepResult;
        }
        nextStep = nextStepResult.data.next_step;
        newStatus = nextStep ? (nextStep.status_on_reach || 'Pending') : 'Completed';
        actionType = 'APPROVED';
        stepCompleted = true;
        break;
      }

      case 'reject':
        newStatus = 'Rejected';
        actionType = 'REJECTED';
        break;

      case 'return': {
        const previous = calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, 'RETURN');
        nextStep = previous.status === 'success' ? previous.data.next_step : null;
        newStatus = nextStep ? (nextStep.status_on_reach || 'Pending') : 'Returned to Requester';
        actionType = 'RETURNED';
        break;
      }

      case 'complete': {
        // v9.2: 'complete' finishes a task step and moves on like an approval (conditions included); it used
        // to close the whole ticket from any step
        if (String(currentStep.step_type || '').toLowerCase() !== 'task') {
          return { status: 'error', message: 'Only a task step can be marked complete - approve this step instead' };
        }
        const afterTask = calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, 'APPROVE');
        if (afterTask.status !== 'success') {
          return afterTask;
        }
        nextStep = afterTask.data.next_step;
        newStatus = nextStep ? (nextStep.status_on_reach || 'Pending') : 'Completed';
        actionType = 'COMPLETED';
        stepCompleted = true;
        break;
      }

      default:
        return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid action: ' + action };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    let newStepDueDate = '';
    if (nextStep) {
      const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(nextStep.id);
      if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
        const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
        newStepDueDate = calculateSLADueDate(now, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
      }
    }

    updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId, {
      status: newStatus,
      current_step_id: nextStep ? nextStep.id : '',
      step_due_date: newStepDueDate,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: userId
    });

    createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: actionType,
      comment: payload.comment || `Ticket ${decision === 'complete' ? 'completed' : decision + 'd'}`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: newStatus,
      from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
      to_step_id: nextStep ? nextStep.id : null,
      field_changes: decision === 'approve' ? { role_ids: permission.data.eligible_role_ids } : null
    });

    createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'WORKFLOW_PROGRESSION',
      details: {
        action: decision,
        from_status: ticket.status,
        to_status: newStatus,
        from_step: ticket.current_step_id,
        to_step: nextStep ? nextStep.id : null,
        comment: payload.comment
      }
    });

    const completion = stepCompleted ? processStepCompletion(ticketId, ticket.current_step_id, 'approve') : null;
    triggerWorkflowNotifications(ticketId, nextStep ? nextStep.id : null, decision);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        action_performed: decision,
        new_status: newStatus,
        next_step: nextStep,
        step_due_date: newStepDueDate || null,
        partial: false,
        completion_results: completion && completion.status === 'success' ? completion.data : []
      },
      message: `Ticket ${decision === 'complete' ? 'completed' : decision + 'd'} successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in progressTicketWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Cancel a ticket (v9.0: header-driven; a blank is_active counts as active).
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} reason - Cancellation reason
 * @param {string} userId - User cancelling
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_id, cancelled_at, cancelled_by, reason }, message }
 */
function cancelTicketWithReason(ticketId, reason, userId) {
  try {
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    if (!isActiveFlag(ticket.is_active)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket is already inactive' };
    }
    if (ticket.cancelled_at || ticket.status === 'Cancelled') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket is already cancelled' };
    }
    if (ticket.status === 'Completed') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot cancel completed ticket' };
    }

    // v9.1: comment requirements (spec 3.3) - checked on the reason as given, before the default fills in
    const missingComment = requireCommentFor(ticket, 'cancel', reason);
    if (missingComment) {
      return { status: 'error', message: missingComment };
    }
    reason = String(reason || '').trim() || 'No reason given';

    const now = new Date();
    updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId, {
      status: 'Cancelled',
      cancelled_at: now,
      cancelled_by: userId,
      cancellation_reason: reason,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: userId
    });

    createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: 'CANCELLED',
      comment: `Ticket cancelled: ${reason}`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: 'Cancelled',
      from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id
    });

    createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'TICKET_CANCELLED',
      details: {
        reason: reason,
        previous_status: ticket.status,
        previous_step: ticket.current_step_id
      }
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        cancelled_at: now,
        cancelled_by: userId,
        reason: reason
      },
      message: 'Ticket cancelled successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in cancelTicketWithReason: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Reopen a cancelled or rejected ticket (admin).
 * v9.0: stepId is optional - by default the ticket returns to the step it was rejected/cancelled at
 * (spec 3.4), else the first step. Header-driven write.
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Target step (optional)
 * @param {string} userId - Admin reopening
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_id, reopened_to_step, new_status, step_due_date }, message }
 */
function reopenTicketToStep(ticketId, stepId, userId) {
  try {
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    // v9.1 (review R-04): a ticket returned from the first step ('Returned to Requester', no current step)
    // is resubmitted the same way, back to the step it was returned from
    if (ticket.status !== 'Cancelled' && ticket.status !== 'Rejected' && ticket.status !== 'Returned to Requester') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Can only reopen cancelled, rejected or returned tickets' };
    }

    let targetStepId = stepId;
    if (!targetStepId) {
      const history = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
      const closing = (history ? history.records : [])
        .filter(h => sameId(h.ticket_id, ticketId) && ['REJECTED', 'CANCELLED', 'RETURNED'].includes(h.action) && h.from_step_id)
        .pop();
      if (closing) {
        targetStepId = closing.from_step_id;
      } else {
        const steps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
        targetStepId = steps.status === 'success' && steps.data.length ? steps.data[0].id : null;
      }
    }

    const targetStep = targetStepId ? getWorkflowStepById(targetStepId) : { status: 'error' };
    if (targetStep.status !== 'success') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid target step' };
    }

    const step = targetStep.data;
    if (!sameId(step.ticket_type_id, ticket.ticket_type_id) || !sameId(step.company_id, ticket.company_id)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step does not belong to ticket workflow' };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    let newStepDueDate = '';
    const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(step.id);
    if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
      const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
      newStepDueDate = calculateSLADueDate(now, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
    }

    updateRecordFields(SHEETS.TICKETS, ticketId, {
      status: step.status_on_reach || 'Pending',
      current_step_id: step.id,
      step_due_date: newStepDueDate,
      cancelled_at: '',
      cancelled_by: '',
      cancellation_reason: '',
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: userId
    });

    createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: 'REOPENED',
      comment: `Ticket reopened to step: ${step.name}`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: step.status_on_reach,
      from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
      to_step_id: step.id
    });

    createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'TICKET_REOPENED',
      details: {
        reopened_from: ticket.status,
        reopened_to: step.status_on_reach,
        target_step: step.id,
        step_name: step.name
      }
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        reopened_to_step: step.id,
        new_status: step.status_on_reach,
        step_due_date: newStepDueDate || null
      },
      message: 'Ticket reopened successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reopenTicketToStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * A ticket's history, newest first.
 * v9.1: ids compared as strings (A8) - ticket ids are numbers in the sheet and strings in a GET request, so
 * the strict comparison returned nothing. Same-timestamp rows keep append order (newest first).
 * @param {string|number} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: history rows, message }
 */
function getTicketHistory(ticketId) {
  try {
    if (!ticketId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id is required' };
    }
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
    if (!table) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket history sheet not found' };
    }

    const history = table.records
      .map((row, index) => ({ row, index }))
      .filter(entry => sameId(entry.row.ticket_id, ticketId))
      .sort((a, b) => (new Date(b.row.timestamp) - new Date(a.row.timestamp)) || (b.index - a.index))
      .map(entry => entry.row);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: history,
      message: `Retrieved ${history.length} history entries`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getTicketHistory: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a ticket history entry
 * @param {Object} payload - History entry data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created history entry
 */
function createTicketHistoryEntry(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_history');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket history sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_id', 'user_id', 'action'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const historyId = 'th_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const now = new Date();

    const historyData = {
      id: historyId,
      ticket_id: payload.ticket_id,
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      action: payload.action,
      comment: payload.comment || '',
      from_status: payload.from_status || null,
      to_status: payload.to_status || null,
      from_step_id: payload.from_step_id || null,
      to_step_id: payload.to_step_id || null,
      field_changes: payload.field_changes ? JSON.stringify(payload.field_changes) : null,
      timestamp: now
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(historyData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: historyData,
      message: 'History entry created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketHistoryEntry: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get detailed system action logs for a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with action logs data
 */
function getTicketActionLogs(ticketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_action_logs');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket action logs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const logs = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const logEntry = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        logEntry[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (logEntry.ticket_id === ticketId) {
        // Parse details JSON if it exists
        if (logEntry.details && typeof logEntry.details === 'string') {
          try {
            logEntry.details = JSON.parse(logEntry.details);
          } catch (e) {
            // Keep as string if not valid JSON
          }
        }
        logs.push(logEntry);
      }
    }

    // Sort by timestamp (most recent first)
    logs.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp) - new Date(a.timestamp));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: logs,
      message: `Retrieved ${logs.length} action log entries`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getTicketActionLogs: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a ticket action log entry
 * @param {Object} payload - Action log data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created log entry
 */
function createTicketActionLog(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_action_logs');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket action logs sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_id', 'user_id', 'action_type'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const logId = 'tal_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const now = new Date();

    const logData = {
      id: logId,
      ticket_id: payload.ticket_id,
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      action_type: payload.action_type,
      details: payload.details ? JSON.stringify(payload.details) : null,
      timestamp: now
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(logData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: logData,
      message: 'Action log entry created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketActionLog: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Next step for an action: APPROVE -> the step the current step's conditions route to, else the following
 * step (null = workflow complete); RETURN -> the step the ticket came from (null = back to the requester).
 * v9.0: step ids compared as strings (A8).
 * v9.2: conditional routing (routeByConditions; a route can also end the workflow) and returns follow the
 * path in ticket_history (previousVisitedStep), falling back to the step order when history doesn't show it.
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} currentAction - APPROVE | RETURN | REJECT | CANCEL
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { current_step, next_step, is_workflow_complete, routed_by }, message }
 */
function calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, currentAction) {
  try {
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
    if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success') {
      return workflowSteps;
    }

    const steps = workflowSteps.data;
    const currentStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => sameId(step.id, ticket.current_step_id));
    if (currentStepIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Current step not found in workflow' };
    }

    const currentStep = steps[currentStepIndex];
    let nextStep = null;
    let routedBy = null;

    switch (String(currentAction).toUpperCase()) {
      case 'APPROVE': {
        const route = routeByConditions(currentStep, ticket, steps);
        if (route.matched) {
          nextStep = route.end ? null : route.step;
          routedBy = { group: route.group, is_default: route.is_default, ends_workflow: route.end };
        } else {
          nextStep = currentStepIndex + 1 < steps.length ? steps[currentStepIndex + 1] : null;
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'RETURN': {
        const previous = previousVisitedStep(ticketId, currentStep.id, steps);
        nextStep = previous.found ? previous.step : (currentStepIndex > 0 ? steps[currentStepIndex - 1] : null);
        break;
      }
      case 'REJECT':
      case 'CANCEL':
        nextStep = null;
        break;
      default:
        return { status: 'error', message: 'Unknown action for workflow calculation' };
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        current_step: currentStep,
        next_step: nextStep,
        is_workflow_complete: nextStep === null && String(currentAction).toUpperCase() === 'APPROVE',
        routed_by: routedBy
      },
      message: nextStep ? `Next step: ${nextStep.name}` : 'Workflow complete'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in calculateNextWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate if a workflow transition is allowed
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} targetStepId - Target step ID
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateWorkflowTransition(ticketId, targetStepId) {
  try {
    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Get workflow steps
    const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
    if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success') {
      return workflowSteps;
    }

    const steps = workflowSteps.data;
    const currentStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === ticket.current_step_id);
    const targetStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === targetStepId);

    const issues = [];

    if (currentStepIndex === -1) {
      issues.push('Current step not found in workflow');
    }

    if (targetStepIndex === -1) {
      issues.push('Target step not found in workflow');
    }

    if (issues.length === 0) {
      // Check if transition is valid (can only go to adjacent steps or return to previous)
      const stepDifference = targetStepIndex - currentStepIndex;

      if (stepDifference === 1) {
        // Moving forward - always allowed
      } else if (stepDifference === -1) {
        // Moving backward (return) - allowed
      } else if (stepDifference === 0) {
        issues.push('Cannot transition to the same step');
      } else {
        issues.push('Can only transition to adjacent steps');
      }
    }

    // Check ticket state
    if (!ticket.is_active) {
      issues.push('Cannot transition inactive ticket');
    }

    if (ticket.cancelled_at) {
      issues.push('Cannot transition cancelled ticket');
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        is_valid: issues.length === 0,
        issues: issues,
        current_step_index: currentStepIndex,
        target_step_index: targetStepIndex
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Workflow transition is valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateWorkflowTransition: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Approvers for a ticket's current step, with the approvals of this visit.
 * v9.1: takes the already-loaded ticket so an approval doesn't re-read the tickets tab (review R-08).
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record (optional - read when not given)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, approver_logic, eligible_users, user_count, required_role_ids,
 *   approved_role_ids, approvals }, message }
 */
function getApproversForCurrentStep(ticketId, ticket = null) {
  try {
    if (!ticket) {
      const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
      if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
        return currentTicket;
      }
      ticket = currentTicket.data;
    }

    if (!ticket.current_step_id) {
      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: { step_id: null, approver_logic: 'any', eligible_users: [], user_count: 0,
          required_role_ids: [], approved_role_ids: [], approvals: [] },
        message: 'No current step - ticket may be completed or cancelled'
      };
    }

    const stepUsers = getUsersForStep(ticket.current_step_id, ticket);
    if (stepUsers.status !== 'success') {
      return stepUsers;
    }

    const stepDetails = getWorkflowStepById(ticket.current_step_id);
    const approverLogic = stepDetails.status === 'success' ? (stepDetails.data.approver_logic || 'any') : 'any';
    const approvals = getStepVisitApprovals(ticket.id || ticketId, ticket.current_step_id);
    const required = ((getStepApprovers(ticket.current_step_id).data) || []).map(a => String(a.role_id));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
        approver_logic: approverLogic,
        eligible_users: stepUsers.data,
        user_count: unique(stepUsers.data.map(u => String(u.user_id))).length,
        required_role_ids: required,
        approved_role_ids: unique([].concat(...approvals.map(a => a.role_ids))),
        approvals: approvals
      },
      message: `Found ${stepUsers.data.length} eligible approvers for current step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getApproversForCurrentStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Can this user act on the ticket's current step?
 * v9.0: the user needs an active assignment for one of the step's approver roles in the ticket's
 * company/type; under approver_logic 'all' a user who already approved this visit of the step can't again.
 * v9.1 (review R-03): can_act = may reject / return. A partial approver under 'all' can still stop the
 * ticket; can_approve stays false for them. Takes the loaded ticket (R-08).
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record (optional)
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { can_approve, can_act, user_id, step_id, approver_logic, already_approved,
 *   eligible_role_ids, required_role_ids, approved_role_ids }, message }
 */
function checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticketId, ticket = null) {
  try {
    const current = getApproversForCurrentStep(ticketId, ticket);
    if (current.status !== 'success') {
      return current;
    }

    const info = current.data;
    const eligibleRoleIds = unique(info.eligible_users.filter(u => sameId(u.user_id, userId)).map(u => String(u.role_id)));
    const alreadyApproved = info.approvals.some(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId));
    const needsAll = String(info.approver_logic).toLowerCase() === 'all';
    const canAct = Boolean(info.step_id) && eligibleRoleIds.length > 0;
    const canApprove = canAct && !(needsAll && alreadyApproved);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        can_approve: canApprove,
        can_act: canAct,
        user_id: userId,
        step_id: info.step_id,
        approver_logic: info.approver_logic,
        already_approved: alreadyApproved,
        eligible_role_ids: eligibleRoleIds,
        required_role_ids: info.required_role_ids,
        approved_role_ids: info.approved_role_ids
      },
      message: canApprove ? 'User can approve this step' : (canAct ? 'User can reject or return this step' : 'User cannot approve this step')
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in checkUserCanApproveStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Process step completion (handle chained workflows, external integrations)
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Completed step ID
 * @param {string} action - Action that completed the step
 * @returns {Object} Response with processing results
 */
function processStepCompletion(ticketId, stepId, action) {
  try {
    // Get step details
    const stepDetails = getWorkflowStepById(stepId);
    if (stepDetails.status !== 'success') {
      return stepDetails;
    }

    const step = stepDetails.data;
    const results = [];

    // Handle chained workflow (create linked ticket)
    if (step.next_ticket_type_id && action.toLowerCase() === 'approve') {
      // Get current ticket details for chaining
      const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
      if (currentTicket.status === 'success') {
        const chainedTicketPayload = {
          title: `Chained from: ${currentTicket.data.title}`,
          ticket_type_id: step.next_ticket_type_id,
          requester_id: currentTicket.data.requester_id,
          company_id: currentTicket.data.company_id,
          parent_ticket_id: ticketId
        };

        const chainedResult = createTicketWithWorkflow(chainedTicketPayload);
        if (chainedResult.status === 'success') {
          // Create ticket link
          // v9.2: link_type 'chained' + company, so the dashboard can show the child under its parent
          createTicketLink(ticketId, chainedResult.data.id, {
            link_type: 'chained',
            company_id: currentTicket.data.company_id,
            description: `Created by workflow step: ${step.name}`
          });
          results.push({
            type: 'CHAINED_TICKET',
            success: true,
            data: chainedResult.data
          });
        } else {
          results.push({
            type: 'CHAINED_TICKET',
            success: false,
            error: chainedResult.message
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // Handle external app integration
    if (step.external_app_url) {
      // This would typically make an HTTP request to external system
      // For now, just log the integration point
      Logger.log(`External integration required: ${step.external_app_url}`);
      results.push({
        type: 'EXTERNAL_INTEGRATION',
        success: true,
        data: { url: step.external_app_url }
      });
    }

    // Handle completion actions
    if (step.completion_action_name) {
      Logger.log(`Completion action required: ${step.completion_action_name}`);
      results.push({
        type: 'COMPLETION_ACTION',
        success: true,
        data: { action: step.completion_action_name }
      });
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: results,
      message: `Processed ${results.length} step completion actions`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in processStepCompletion: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Trigger workflow notifications for step changes
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - New step ID (null if workflow complete)
 * @param {string} action - Action that triggered the change
 * @returns {Object} Response with notification results
 */
function triggerWorkflowNotifications(ticketId, stepId, action) {
  try {
    const notifications = [];

    // Get ticket details
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Notify requester of status change
    notifications.push({
      type: 'STATUS_CHANGE',
      recipient: ticket.requester_id,
      message: `Ticket ${ticket.ticket_number}: ${action}d`,
      ticket_id: ticketId
    });

    // If there's a new step, notify the approvers
    if (stepId) {
      const stepApprovers = getUsersForStep(stepId);
      if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
        stepApprovers.data.forEach(approver => {
          notifications.push({
            type: 'APPROVAL_REQUEST',
            recipient: approver.user_id,
            message: `New approval required for ticket ${ticket.ticket_number}`,
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            step_id: stepId
          });
        });
      }
    }

    // Log notifications (in a real system, these would be sent via email/SMS/push)
    notifications.forEach(notification => {
      Logger.log(`NOTIFICATION: ${notification.type} to ${notification.recipient} - ${notification.message}`);
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: notifications,
      message: `Triggered ${notifications.length} notifications`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in triggerWorkflowNotifications: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}
