/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PHASE 13.0: CORE TICKET LOOP + WORKFLOW ADMIN (v9.1)
 * Handlers behind the v9.1 routes: ticket numbers, workflow step admin (create / copy / default), custom
 * field get, ticket links, company code lock, comment requirements, ticket history and the approver inbox.
 * Plan: ~/.claude/plans/plan-already-the-next-squishy-sutherland.md (Release 2)
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Cell value -> boolean: only true / 'TRUE' count. Unlike isActiveFlag a blank is false, and so is anything
 * else - e.g. the dates the pre-v9.1 updateCompany wrote into code_locked.
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function boolFlag(value) {
  return value === true || String(value).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
}

/** Is a company's code locked? (boolFlag - a date left in code_locked by the old bug reads as unlocked) */
function isLockedFlag(value) {
  return boolFlag(value);
}

/**
 * Check an SLA before it is written.
 * @returns {string|null} Error message, or null when valid
 */
function validateStepSLA(duration, unit) {
  const value = Number(duration);
  if (!isFinite(value) || value <= 0) return 'SLA duration must be a positive number';
  if (!['hours', 'days'].includes(String(unit || '').toLowerCase())) return 'SLA unit must be "hours" or "days"';
  return null;
}

/**
 * Create or update a step's SLA (one active SLA per step).
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Object} payload - { sla_duration, sla_unit, exclude_weekends }
 * @param {string} userId - Acting user
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: sla, message }
 */
function upsertStepSLA(stepId, payload, userId) {
  const error = validateStepSLA(payload.sla_duration, payload.sla_unit);
  if (error) return { status: 'error', message: error };

  const now = new Date();
  const values = {
    duration: Number(payload.sla_duration),
    unit: String(payload.sla_unit).toLowerCase(),
    exclude_weekends: boolFlag(payload.exclude_weekends),
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: userId
  };

  const existing = getStepSLAs(stepId);
  if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
    const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS, existing.data[0].id, values);
    return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'SLA updated' };
  }

  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS);
  const sla = { id: generateId('sla'), step_id: stepId, ...values, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: userId };
  sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), sla));
  return { status: 'success', data: sla, message: 'SLA created' };
}

/**
 * Soft-delete a step's active SLAs (deleted_* columns, as step_slas has).
 * @returns {number} How many SLAs were removed
 */
function removeStepSLA(stepId, userId, reason) {
  const existing = getStepSLAs(stepId);
  const slas = existing.status === 'success' ? existing.data : [];
  const now = new Date();
  slas.forEach(sla => updateRecordFields(SHEETS.STEP_SLAS, sla.id, {
    is_active: false,
    deleted_at: now,
    deleted_by: userId,
    deletion_reason: reason,
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: userId
  }));
  return slas.length;
}

/**
 * Is the user an admin? Same rule as the frontend (UserContext: a role whose name contains "admin"), over
 * the user's assignments that are active today.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isAdminUser(userId) {
  if (!userId) return false;
  const assignments = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const roles = readTable(SHEETS.ROLES);
  const now = new Date();
  const roleIds = (assignments ? assignments.records : [])
    .filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId) && isAssignmentActive(a, now))
    .map(a => String(a.role_id));
  return (roles ? roles.records : []).some(r =>
    roleIds.includes(String(r.id)) && isActiveFlag(r.is_active) && String(r.name || '').toLowerCase().includes('admin'));
}

// --- Ticket numbers ----------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Company and ticket type codes for numbering.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { company, ticketType } }
 */
function resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  if (!companyId || !ticketTypeId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'company_id and ticket_type_id are required' };
  }
  const company = findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId);
  const ticketType = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeId);
  if (!company) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  if (!ticketType) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${ticketTypeId} not found` };
  if (!company.record.code || !ticketType.record.code) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Company and ticket type both need a code for ticket numbering' };
  }
  return { status: 'success', data: { company: company.record, ticketType: ticketType.record } };
}

/**
 * getNextTicketNumber route: the number the next ticket would get, without reserving it (read-only).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_number, sequence_name, next_sequence } }
 */
function peekTicketNumber(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  const codes = resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId);
  if (codes.status !== 'success') return codes;

  const sequenceName = `${codes.data.company.code}-${codes.data.ticketType.code}`;
  const counters = readTable(SHEETS.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS);
  const counter = (counters ? counters.records : []).find(c => String(c.sequence_name) === sequenceName);
  const next = (counter ? parseInt(counter.last_number, 10) || 0 : 0) + 1;
  const year = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Manila', 'yyyy');

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      ticket_number: `${sequenceName}-${year}-${String(next).padStart(8, '0')}`,
      sequence_name: sequenceName,
      next_sequence: next
    }
  };
}

/**
 * generateTicketNumber route: reserve the next number (increments the counter).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_number } }
 */
function generateTicketNumberAction(companyId, ticketTypeId) {
  const codes = resolveNumberingCodes(companyId, ticketTypeId);
  if (codes.status !== 'success') return codes;
  return { status: 'success', data: { ticket_number: generateTicketNumber(codes.data.company.code, codes.data.ticketType.code) } };
}

/** getTicketType route. */
function getTicketTypeById(ticketTypeId) {
  if (!ticketTypeId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeId);
  return found
    ? { status: 'success', data: { ...found.record, features: ticketTypeFeatures(found.record) } }
    : { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${ticketTypeId} not found` };
}

/** getCustomField route. */
function getCustomFieldById(fieldId) {
  if (!fieldId) return { status: 'error', message: 'field_id is required' };
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS, fieldId);
  return found && isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active)
    ? { status: 'success', data: found.record }
    : { status: 'error', message: `Custom field ${fieldId} not found` };
}

// --- Workflow admin ----------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * copyWorkflowSteps route: copy a company's workflow for a ticket type to another company - steps (new ids),
 * approver roles, SLAs and step conditions (step_id / next_step_id remapped). Spec 3.0.1: copy-based setup,
 * so the target must not have steps yet (each company then owns and edits its copy).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { copied_steps, approvers, slas, conditions, step_id_map }, message }
 */
function copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany(ticketTypeId, sourceCompanyId, targetCompanyId, userId = null) {
  try {
    if (!ticketTypeId || !sourceCompanyId || !targetCompanyId) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id, source_company_id and target_company_id are required' };
    }
    if (sameId(sourceCompanyId, targetCompanyId)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Source and target company are the same' };
    }
    if (!findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, targetCompanyId)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${targetCompanyId} not found` };
    }

    const source = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, sourceCompanyId);
    if (source.status !== 'success') return source;
    if (source.data.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'The source company has no workflow steps for this ticket type' };
    }
    const target = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, targetCompanyId);
    if (target.status === 'success' && target.data.length > 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: `The target company already has ${target.data.length} workflow step(s) for this ticket type - edit or delete them instead` };
    }

    const actor = userId || getCurrentUserId();
    const now = new Date();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const stepSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
    const stepHeaders = sheetHeaders(stepSheet);
    const idMap = {};

    source.data.forEach(step => {
      const newId = generateId('ws');
      idMap[String(step.id)] = newId;
      stepSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(stepHeaders, {
        id: newId,
        ticket_type_id: step.ticket_type_id,
        company_id: targetCompanyId,
        name: step.name,
        status_on_reach: step.status_on_reach,
        step_type: step.step_type,
        approver_logic: step.approver_logic,
        sort_order: step.sort_order,
        next_ticket_type_id: step.next_ticket_type_id,
        external_app_url: step.external_app_url,
        completion_action_name: step.completion_action_name,
        is_active: true,
        created_at: now,
        created_by: actor,
        updated_at: now,
        updated_by: actor
      }));
    });

    // Approver roles
    const approverTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    const approverSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
    const approverHeaders = sheetHeaders(approverSheet);
    let approvers = 0;
    (approverTable ? approverTable.records : []).forEach(row => {
      const newStepId = idMap[String(row.step_id)];
      if (!newStepId) return;
      approverSheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(approverHeaders, { step_id: newStepId, role_id: row.role_id }));
      approvers++;
    });

    // SLAs (getWorkflowSteps attached the active one to each step)
    let slas = 0;
    source.data.forEach(step => {
      if (step.sla_duration === '' || step.sla_duration === undefined || step.sla_duration === null) return;
      const result = upsertStepSLA(idMap[String(step.id)], {
        sla_duration: step.sla_duration,
        sla_unit: step.sla_unit,
        exclude_weekends: step.exclude_weekends
      }, actor);
      if (result.status === 'success') slas++;
    });

    // Conditions
    const conditionTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS);
    let conditions = 0;
    if (conditionTable) {
      const conditionHeaders = sheetHeaders(conditionTable.sheet);
      conditionTable.records
        .filter(c => idMap[String(c.step_id)] && isActiveFlag(c.is_active) && !c.deactivated_at)
        .forEach(c => {
          conditionTable.sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(conditionHeaders, {
            ...c,
            id: generateId('sc'),
            step_id: idMap[String(c.step_id)],
            next_step_id: c.next_step_id
              ? (String(c.next_step_id) === STEP_CONDITION_END ? STEP_CONDITION_END : (idMap[String(c.next_step_id)] || ''))
              : '',
            company_id: targetCompanyId,
            is_active: true,
            created_at: now,
            created_by: actor,
            updated_at: now,
            updated_by: actor,
            deactivated_at: '',
            deactivated_by: '',
            deactivation_reason: ''
          }));
          conditions++;
        });
    }

    logAdminAction(actor, 'COPY_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_step', ticketTypeId, {
      source_company_id: sourceCompanyId,
      target_company_id: targetCompanyId,
      steps: source.data.length,
      approvers: approvers,
      slas: slas,
      conditions: conditions
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { copied_steps: source.data.length, approvers: approvers, slas: slas, conditions: conditions, step_id_map: idMap },
      message: `Copied ${source.data.length} step(s), ${approvers} approver role(s), ${slas} SLA(s) and ${conditions} condition(s)`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in copyWorkflowStepsFromCompany: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * createDefaultWorkflow route: one editable approval step, created only on an explicit admin click
 * (spec 3.0.1 - there are no automatic default workflows). The admin still assigns its approver roles.
 * @returns {Object} createWorkflowStep result
 */
function createDefaultWorkflow(ticketTypeId, companyId, userId = null) {
  if (!ticketTypeId || !companyId) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id and company_id are required' };
  }
  const existing = getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, companyId);
  if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `This company already has ${existing.data.length} workflow step(s) for this ticket type` };
  }
  const created = createWorkflowStep({
    ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
    company_id: companyId,
    name: 'For Approval of Approver (1)',
    status_on_reach: 'Pending Approval',
    step_type: 'approval',
    approver_logic: 'any',
    sort_order: 1,
    user_id: userId
  });
  if (created.status === 'success') {
    created.message = 'Default workflow created - assign approver roles to its step';
  }
  return created;
}

// --- Ticket links --------------------------------------------------------------

/** Ticket record by its user-facing number (case-insensitive), or null. */
function findTicketByNumber(tickets, ticketNumber) {
  const wanted = String(ticketNumber || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!wanted) return null;
  return tickets.find(t => String(t.ticket_number || '').trim().toUpperCase() === wanted) || null;
}

/** Link types that order work (a cycle among them can never finish). */
const BLOCKING_LINK_TYPES = ['blocks', 'depends_on'];

/**
 * Path of blocking links from one ticket to another (ticket ids), or null when there is none.
 * @param {Array} links - Active link records
 */
function findBlockingPath(links, fromId, toId) {
  const edges = {};
  links.filter(l => BLOCKING_LINK_TYPES.includes(String(l.link_type))).forEach(l => {
    const key = String(l.parent_ticket_id);
    (edges[key] = edges[key] || []).push(String(l.child_ticket_id));
  });

  const queue = [[String(fromId)]];
  const seen = {};
  while (queue.length) {
    const path = queue.shift();
    const last = path[path.length - 1];
    if (last === String(toId)) return path;
    if (seen[last]) continue;
    seen[last] = true;
    (edges[last] || []).forEach(next => queue.push(path.concat(next)));
  }
  return null;
}

/** Active ticket link records. */
function activeTicketLinks() {
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_LINKS);
  return (table ? table.records : []).filter(l => isActiveFlag(l.is_active) && !l.deactivated_at);
}

/**
 * getTicketLinks route: active links with both tickets' number, title, status and company.
 * Filters: ticket_number (either side), company_id, ticket_type_id, status (either ticket), include_linked
 * (with ticket_number: also links among the tickets connected to it).
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: links }
 */
function getTicketLinksAction(params = {}) {
  const ticketTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const tickets = ticketTable ? ticketTable.records : [];
  const byId = {};
  tickets.forEach(t => { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });

  const view = link => {
    const parent = byId[String(link.parent_ticket_id)] || {};
    const child = byId[String(link.child_ticket_id)] || {};
    return {
      ...link,
      enforce_blocking_rules: boolFlag(link.enforce_blocking_rules),
      sync_status: boolFlag(link.sync_status),
      notify_on_update: boolFlag(link.notify_on_update),
      parent_ticket_number: parent.ticket_number || '',
      parent_title: parent.title || '',
      parent_status: parent.status || '',
      parent_company_id: parent.company_id || '',
      child_ticket_number: child.ticket_number || '',
      child_title: child.title || '',
      child_status: child.status || '',
      child_company_id: child.company_id || ''
    };
  };

  let links = activeTicketLinks().map(view);

  if (params.ticket_number) {
    const ticket = findTicketByNumber(tickets, params.ticket_number);
    if (!ticket) return { status: 'success', data: [], message: `No ticket ${params.ticket_number}` };
    const connected = { [String(ticket.id)]: true };
    if (boolFlag(params.include_linked)) {
      let grew = true;
      while (grew) {
        grew = false;
        links.forEach(l => {
          const p = String(l.parent_ticket_id);
          const c = String(l.child_ticket_id);
          if (connected[p] !== connected[c]) {
            connected[p] = true;
            connected[c] = true;
            grew = true;
          }
        });
      }
    }
    links = links.filter(l => connected[String(l.parent_ticket_id)] || connected[String(l.child_ticket_id)]);
  }
  if (params.company_id) {
    links = links.filter(l => sameId(l.parent_company_id, params.company_id) || sameId(l.child_company_id, params.company_id));
  }
  if (params.ticket_type_id) {
    links = links.filter(l =>
      sameId((byId[String(l.parent_ticket_id)] || {}).ticket_type_id, params.ticket_type_id) ||
      sameId((byId[String(l.child_ticket_id)] || {}).ticket_type_id, params.ticket_type_id));
  }
  if (params.status) {
    const status = String(params.status).toLowerCase();
    links = links.filter(l => String(l.parent_status).toLowerCase() === status || String(l.child_status).toLowerCase() === status);
  }

  return { status: 'success', data: links, message: `Retrieved ${links.length} ticket links` };
}

/**
 * createTicketLink route: link two tickets by number. Refuses a self link, a duplicate (same tickets and
 * type) and a blocks / depends_on link that would close a cycle.
 * @param {Object} payload - { parent_ticket_number, child_ticket_number, link_type, description,
 *   enforce_blocking_rules, sync_status, notify_on_update, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: link, message }
 */
function createTicketLinkAction(payload) {
  const linkType = String(payload.link_type || 'related_to');
  if (!['blocks', 'depends_on', 'related_to', 'duplicates'].includes(linkType)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `Unknown link_type: ${linkType}` };
  }

  const ticketTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const tickets = ticketTable ? ticketTable.records : [];
  const parent = payload.parent_ticket_id ? tickets.find(t => sameId(t.id, payload.parent_ticket_id)) : findTicketByNumber(tickets, payload.parent_ticket_number);
  const child = payload.child_ticket_id ? tickets.find(t => sameId(t.id, payload.child_ticket_id)) : findTicketByNumber(tickets, payload.child_ticket_number);
  if (!parent) return { status: 'error', message: `Parent ticket ${payload.parent_ticket_number || payload.parent_ticket_id} not found` };
  if (!child) return { status: 'error', message: `Child ticket ${payload.child_ticket_number || payload.child_ticket_id} not found` };
  if (sameId(parent.id, child.id)) return { status: 'error', message: 'A ticket cannot be linked to itself' };

  const links = activeTicketLinks();
  const duplicate = links.some(l => sameId(l.parent_ticket_id, parent.id) && sameId(l.child_ticket_id, child.id) && String(l.link_type) === linkType);
  if (duplicate) return { status: 'error', message: 'These tickets already have this link' };

  if (BLOCKING_LINK_TYPES.includes(linkType)) {
    const back = findBlockingPath(links, child.id, parent.id);
    if (back) {
      const numbers = back.concat(String(child.id)).map(id => (tickets.find(t => sameId(t.id, id)) || {}).ticket_number || id);
      return { status: 'error', message: `This link would create a circular dependency: ${numbers.join(' → ')}` };
    }
  }

  const created = createTicketLink(parent.id, child.id, {
    link_type: linkType,
    description: payload.description,
    company_id: parent.company_id,
    enforce_blocking_rules: payload.enforce_blocking_rules,
    sync_status: payload.sync_status,
    notify_on_update: payload.notify_on_update,
    user_id: payload.user_id
  });
  if (created.status !== 'success') return created;

  logAdminAction(payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId(), 'CREATE_TICKET_LINK', 'ticket_link', created.data.id, {
    parent: parent.ticket_number, child: child.ticket_number, link_type: linkType
  });
  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { ...created.data, parent_ticket_number: parent.ticket_number, child_ticket_number: child.ticket_number },
    message: 'Ticket link created successfully'
  };
}

/** deleteTicketLink route: soft delete (A4). */
function deleteTicketLinkAction(linkId, reason) {
  if (!linkId) return { status: 'error', message: 'link_id is required' };
  return deactivateRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_LINKS, linkId, reason || 'Ticket link removed');
}

/**
 * validateTicketDependencies route (read-only): blocking cycles and links whose ticket is missing, inactive
 * or cancelled.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { total_links, circular_dependencies: [[ticket numbers]],
 *   orphaned_tickets: [{ link_id, parent_ticket_number, child_ticket_number, issue }], validation_passed } }
 */
function validateTicketDependencies() {
  const ticketTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const tickets = ticketTable ? ticketTable.records : [];
  const byId = {};
  tickets.forEach(t => { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
  const numberOf = id => (byId[String(id)] || {}).ticket_number || String(id);

  const links = activeTicketLinks();
  const orphaned = [];
  links.forEach(l => {
    [['parent', l.parent_ticket_id], ['child', l.child_ticket_id]].forEach(([side, id]) => {
      const ticket = byId[String(id)];
      let issue = null;
      if (!ticket) issue = `${side} ticket ${id} not found`;
      else if (!isActiveFlag(ticket.is_active)) issue = `${side} ticket ${ticket.ticket_number} is inactive`;
      else if (ticket.cancelled_at || ticket.status === 'Cancelled') issue = `${side} ticket ${ticket.ticket_number} is cancelled`;
      if (issue) {
        orphaned.push({ link_id: l.id, parent_ticket_number: numberOf(l.parent_ticket_id), child_ticket_number: numberOf(l.child_ticket_id), issue: issue });
      }
    });
  });

  // A cycle exists through link l when its child leads back to its parent
  const cycles = [];
  const seenCycles = {};
  links.filter(l => BLOCKING_LINK_TYPES.includes(String(l.link_type))).forEach(l => {
    const path = findBlockingPath(links, l.child_ticket_id, l.parent_ticket_id);
    if (!path) return;
    const cycle = [String(l.parent_ticket_id)].concat(path);
    const key = cycle.slice(0, -1).slice().sort().join('|');
    if (seenCycles[key]) return;
    seenCycles[key] = true;
    cycles.push(cycle.map(numberOf));
  });

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      total_links: links.length,
      circular_dependencies: cycles,
      orphaned_tickets: orphaned,
      validation_passed: cycles.length === 0 && orphaned.length === 0
    },
    message: `${links.length} links: ${cycles.length} cycle(s), ${orphaned.length} orphaned link end(s)`
  };
}

// --- Company code lock (spec 3.3) --------------------------------------------

/** lockCompany route. Idempotent. */
function lockCompany(companyId, reason, userId = null) {
  if (!companyId) return { status: 'error', message: 'id is required' };
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId);
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  if (isLockedFlag(found.record.code_locked)) {
    return { status: 'success', data: { ...found.record, already_locked: true }, message: 'Company code is already locked' };
  }
  const actor = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId, {
    code_locked: true,
    code_locked_at: now,
    code_locked_reason: reason || 'admin_lock',
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: actor
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'LOCK_COMPANY_CODE', 'company', companyId, { code: found.record.code, reason: reason || 'admin_lock' });
  return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'Company code locked' };
}

/**
 * unlockCompany route - admin override (spec 3.3), so a reason is required and it is logged with the
 * previous lock. Changing the code afterwards changes the numbers of new tickets only.
 */
function unlockCompany(companyId, reason, userId = null) {
  if (!companyId) return { status: 'error', message: 'id is required' };
  if (!String(reason || '').trim()) return { status: 'error', message: 'A reason is required to unlock a company code' };
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId);
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  if (!isLockedFlag(found.record.code_locked)) {
    return { status: 'success', data: { ...found.record, already_unlocked: true }, message: 'Company code is not locked' };
  }
  const actor = userId || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const result = updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId, {
    code_locked: false,
    code_locked_at: '',
    code_locked_reason: '',
    updated_at: now,
    updated_by: actor
  });
  logAdminAction(actor, 'UNLOCK_COMPANY_CODE', 'company', companyId, {
    code: found.record.code,
    reason: String(reason).trim(),
    previous_lock: { at: found.record.code_locked_at, reason: found.record.code_locked_reason },
    ticket_count: found.record.ticket_count
  });
  return { status: 'success', data: result.after, message: 'Company code unlocked' };
}

/** checkCompanyLockStatus route (read-only). */
function checkCompanyLockStatus(companyId) {
  if (!companyId) return { status: 'error', message: 'id is required' };
  const found = findRecord(SHEETS.COMPANIES, companyId);
  if (!found) return { status: 'error', message: `Company ${companyId} not found` };
  const c = found.record;
  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      company_id: c.id,
      code: c.code,
      code_locked: isLockedFlag(c.code_locked),
      code_locked_at: isLockedFlag(c.code_locked) ? c.code_locked_at : '',
      code_locked_reason: isLockedFlag(c.code_locked) ? c.code_locked_reason : '',
      ticket_count: Number(c.ticket_count) || 0
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Repair the company lock columns (editor-only - not routed, A4). Before v9.1 every company edit wrote
 * created_at / updated_at into code_locked / code_locked_at, and nothing ever set the lock or ticket_count.
 * For each company: clear a non-boolean code_locked (and its code_locked_at), recount ticket_count from
 * tickets, and lock companies that have tickets ('first_ticket_created (repair)').
 * @param {boolean} apply - false (default) = report only
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { applied, changes: [{ company_id, code, before, after }] } }
 */
function repairCompanyLocks(apply = false) {
  return withScriptLock(() => {
    const companies = readTable(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
    const tickets = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
    if (!companies) return { status: 'error', message: 'Companies sheet not found' };
    const ticketRecords = tickets ? tickets.records : [];

    const changes = [];
    companies.records.forEach(c => {
      const own = ticketRecords.filter(t => sameId(t.company_id, c.id));
      const count = own.length;
      const raw = c.code_locked;
      const validFlag = raw === true || raw === false || raw === '' || ['TRUE', 'FALSE'].includes(String(raw).trim().toUpperCase());
      const locked = validFlag && boolFlag(raw);

      const after = {};
      if (!validFlag) {
        after.code_locked = false;
        after.code_locked_at = '';
      }
      if ((Number(c.ticket_count) || 0) !== count || c.ticket_count === '') after.ticket_count = count;
      if (count > 0 && !locked) {
        const first = own.map(t => toDate(t.created_at)).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => a - b)[0];
        after.code_locked = true;
        after.code_locked_at = (first || new Date()).toISOString();
        after.code_locked_reason = 'first_ticket_created (repair)';
      }
      if (Object.keys(after).length === 0) return;

      changes.push({
        company_id: c.id,
        code: c.code,
        before: { code_locked: raw, code_locked_at: c.code_locked_at, ticket_count: c.ticket_count },
        after: after
      });
      if (apply) updateRecordFields(SHEETS.COMPANIES, c.id, after);
    });

    if (apply && changes.length) {
      logAdminAction(getCurrentUserId(), 'REPAIR_COMPANY_LOCKS', 'company', 'all', { companies: changes.length });
    }
    Logger.log(`repairCompanyLocks(${apply ? 'apply' : 'dry run'}): ${changes.length} company row(s) ${apply ? 'repaired' : 'to repair'}`);
    changes.forEach(ch => Logger.log(`  ${ch.code} (${ch.company_id}): ${JSON.stringify(ch.before)} -> ${JSON.stringify(ch.after)}`));
    return { status: 'success', data: { applied: apply, changes: changes } };
  });
}

/** Apply repairCompanyLocks (no-argument wrapper for the Apps Script editor). */
function repairCompanyLocksApply() {
  return repairCompanyLocks(true);
}

// --- Comment requirements (spec 3.3) ------------------------------------------

const COMMENT_REQUIREMENT_KEYS = ['require_on_approve', 'require_on_return', 'require_on_reject', 'require_on_cancel'];

/**
 * getCommentRequirements route: which actions need a comment for a ticket type. A row for the company wins
 * over a global row (blank company_id); no row = nothing required.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_type_id, company_id, source, require_on_* } }
 */
function getCommentRequirements(ticketTypeId, companyId = null) {
  if (!ticketTypeId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS);
  const rows = (table ? table.records : []).filter(r => sameId(r.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId) && isActiveFlag(r.is_active));
  const row = (companyId && rows.find(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId))) || rows.find(r => !r.company_id) || null;

  const data = { ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId, company_id: row ? row.company_id || '' : '', source: row ? (row.company_id ? 'company' : 'global') : 'none' };
  COMMENT_REQUIREMENT_KEYS.forEach(key => { data[key] = row ? boolFlag(row[key]) : false; });
  return { status: 'success', data: data };
}

/**
 * saveCommentRequirements route: upsert the row for (ticket_type_id, company_id). comment_requirements has
 * no id column, so the row is matched on that pair and written by header.
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_type_id, company_id, require_on_approve, require_on_return,
 *   require_on_reject, require_on_cancel, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: saved row }
 */
function saveCommentRequirements(payload) {
  if (!payload.ticket_type_id) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };
  if (!findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, payload.ticket_type_id)) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${payload.ticket_type_id} not found` };
  }

  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS);
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS);
  const companyId = payload.company_id || '';
  const index = table.records.findIndex(r => sameId(r.ticket_type_id, payload.ticket_type_id) && sameId(r.company_id || '', companyId));
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();

  const values = {};
  COMMENT_REQUIREMENT_KEYS.forEach(key => { values[key] = boolFlag(payload[key]); });

  let saved;
  if (index >= 0) {
    saved = { ...table.records[index], ...values, is_active: true, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor };
    sheet.getRange(table.rowNumbers[index], 1, 1, table.headers.length).setValues([rowFromRecord(table.headers, saved)]);
  } else {
    saved = { ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id, company_id: companyId, ...values, is_active: true, created_at: now, created_by: actor, updated_at: now, updated_by: actor };
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(sheetHeaders(sheet), saved));
  }

  logAdminAction(actor, 'SAVE_COMMENT_REQUIREMENTS', 'ticket_type', payload.ticket_type_id, { company_id: companyId, ...values });
  return { status: 'success', data: saved, message: 'Comment requirements saved' };
}

/**
 * The error message when an action needs a comment and none was given, else null.
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record
 * @param {string} action - approve | return | reject | cancel
 * @param {string} comment - The user's comment
 */
function requireCommentFor(ticket, action, comment) {
  const key = 'require_on_' + String(action || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!COMMENT_REQUIREMENT_KEYS.includes(key)) return null;
  const reqs = getCommentRequirements(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
  if (reqs.status !== 'success' || !reqs.data[key]) return null;
  return String(comment || '').trim() ? null : `A comment is required to ${String(action).toLowerCase()} this ticket`;
}

// --- Approver inbox -------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * getTicketsForApproval route: open tickets whose current step the user can approve now. Same rules as
 * checkUserCanApproveStep (an active assignment for one of the step's roles in the ticket's company and
 * type; under 'all', not already approved this visit), with each table read once instead of per ticket.
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: [ticket + { current_step_name, approver_logic, eligible_role_ids }] }
 */
function getTicketsForApproval(userId) {
  if (!userId) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };

  const now = new Date();
  const assignmentTable = readTable(SHEETS.USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS);
  const assignments = (assignmentTable ? assignmentTable.records : []).filter(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId) && isAssignmentActive(a, now));
  if (assignments.length === 0) return { status: 'success', data: [], message: 'User has no active role assignments' };

  const approverTable = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_APPROVERS);
  const rolesByStep = {};
  (approverTable ? approverTable.records : []).forEach(a => {
    (rolesByStep[String(a.step_id)] = rolesByStep[String(a.step_id)] || []).push(String(a.role_id));
  });

  const stepTable = readTable(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
  const stepById = {};
  (stepTable ? stepTable.records : []).forEach(s => { stepById[String(s.id)] = s; });

  const ticketTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const historyTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
  const historyRows = historyTable ? historyTable.records : [];

  const inbox = (ticketTable ? ticketTable.records : [])
    .filter(t => t.current_step_id && isActiveFlag(t.is_active) && !t.cancelled_at && !TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status))
    .map(t => {
      const stepRoles = rolesByStep[String(t.current_step_id)] || [];
      const roleIds = unique(assignments
        .filter(a => stepRoles.includes(String(a.role_id)) && sameId(a.company_id, t.company_id) &&
          (!a.ticket_type_id || sameId(a.ticket_type_id, t.ticket_type_id)))
        .map(a => String(a.role_id)));
      if (roleIds.length === 0) return null;

      const step = stepById[String(t.current_step_id)] || {};
      const logic = String(step.approver_logic || 'any').toLowerCase();
      if (logic === 'all') {
        const approvals = visitApprovalsFromRows(historyRows, t.id, t.current_step_id);
        if (approvals.some(a => sameId(a.user_id, userId))) return null;
      }
      return { ...t, current_step_name: step.name || '', approver_logic: logic, eligible_role_ids: roleIds };
    })
    .filter(Boolean);

  return { status: 'success', data: inbox, message: `${inbox.length} ticket(s) waiting for approval` };
}

/**
 * Approvals recorded in the ticket's current visit to a step, from already-read ticket_history rows (in
 * append order). A visit starts at any row that brings the ticket to the step other than an approval
 * recorded at that step - so a reopen to the same step (REOPENED S -> S) starts a new visit (review R-06).
 * @returns {Array} history rows (+ role_ids parsed from field_changes)
 */
function visitApprovalsFromRows(rows, ticketId, stepId) {
  let arrival = -1;
  rows.forEach((row, index) => {
    if (sameId(row.ticket_id, ticketId) && sameId(row.to_step_id, stepId) && row.action !== 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL') {
      arrival = index;
    }
  });

  return rows.slice(arrival + 1)
    .filter(row => sameId(row.ticket_id, ticketId) && sameId(row.from_step_id, stepId) &&
      (row.action === 'PARTIAL_APPROVAL' || row.action === 'APPROVED'))
    .map(row => {
      let roleIds = [];
      try {
        const changes = typeof row.field_changes === 'string' && row.field_changes ? JSON.parse(row.field_changes) : row.field_changes;
        roleIds = ((changes && changes.role_ids) || []).map(String);
      } catch (e) {
        roleIds = [];
      }
      return { ...row, role_ids: roleIds };
    });
}

// =================================================================================
// END OF PHASE 13.0 (v9.1)
// =================================================================================

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * PHASE 14.0: WORKFLOW LOGIC, REPORTS, AUDIT LOG (v9.2)
 * Conditional routing (spec 3.3 "stitching"), step-return along the path the ticket took, the report
 * configuration + report data behind /reports (spec 3.3 / 3.4) and the admin audit log reads (spec 3.5).
 * Plan: ~/.claude/plans/plan-already-the-next-squishy-sutherland.md (v9.2)
 * =================================================================================
 */

// --- Conditional routing --------------------------------------------------------

/** A route target that ends the workflow instead of moving to a step. */
const STEP_CONDITION_END = '__end__';

/** Ticket fields a condition can test besides the ticket type's custom fields (custom_field_id value). */
const STANDARD_CONDITION_FIELDS = { 'field:priority': 'priority', 'field:title': 'title' };

/** Operator spellings -> canonical name (the UI names from src/utils/conditionalWorkflows.js, plus symbols). */
const CONDITION_OPERATOR_ALIASES = {
  '=': 'equals', '==': 'equals', 'eq': 'equals', 'equals': 'equals',
  '!=': 'not_equals', '<>': 'not_equals', 'ne': 'not_equals', 'not_equals': 'not_equals',
  '>': 'greater_than', 'gt': 'greater_than', 'greater_than': 'greater_than',
  '>=': 'greater_than_or_equal', 'gte': 'greater_than_or_equal', 'greater_than_or_equal': 'greater_than_or_equal',
  '<': 'less_than', 'lt': 'less_than', 'less_than': 'less_than',
  '<=': 'less_than_or_equal', 'lte': 'less_than_or_equal', 'less_than_or_equal': 'less_than_or_equal',
  'contains': 'contains', 'not_contains': 'not_contains',
  'in': 'in', 'not_in': 'not_in',
  'is_empty': 'is_empty', 'is_not_empty': 'is_not_empty',
  'regex_match': 'regex_match'
};

/** Canonical operator name, or null when unknown. */
function normalizeConditionOperator(operator) {
  return CONDITION_OPERATOR_ALIASES[String(operator || '').trim().toLowerCase()] || null;
}

/**
 * Active step_conditions rows of a step grouped into routes, in condition_group order. Rows in a group are
 * ANDed; the group's target is its first row's next_step_id. A group whose only row has no field is the
 * default route. Rows written before v9.2 without a condition_group form one group.
 * @returns {Array} [{ group, next_step_id, is_default, rows }]
 */
function stepConditionGroups(stepId, conditionRows = null) {
  const rows = conditionRows || (() => {
    const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS);
    return table ? table.records : [];
  })();
  const groups = {};
  const order = [];
  rows
    .filter(c => sameId(c.step_id, stepId) && isActiveFlag(c.is_active) && !c.deactivated_at)
    .forEach(c => {
      const key = String(c.condition_group === '' || c.condition_group === null || c.condition_group === undefined ? 0 : c.condition_group);
      if (!groups[key]) {
        groups[key] = { group: key, next_step_id: String(c.next_step_id || ''), rows: [] };
        order.push(key);
      }
      groups[key].rows.push(c);
    });
  return order
    .sort((a, b) => (Number(a) - Number(b)) || a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(key => {
      const g = groups[key];
      g.is_default = g.rows.length === 1 && !g.rows[0].custom_field_id;
      return g;
    });
}

/**
 * A ticket's values for condition checks, keyed by custom_field_id (plus the standard fields). Each value
 * carries every spelling it can be matched on - a dropdown value matches its option id, value or label.
 * @returns {Object} { [fieldId]: { type, raw, candidates } }
 */
function ticketConditionValues(ticket) {
  const fieldTable = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);
  const fieldTypes = {};
  (fieldTable ? fieldTable.records : []).forEach(f => { fieldTypes[String(f.id)] = String(f.type || ''); });

  const optionTable = readTable(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);
  const options = {};
  (optionTable ? optionTable.records : []).forEach(o => { options[String(o.id)] = o; });

  const blank = v => v === '' || v === null || v === undefined;
  const values = {};
  const valueTable = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);
  (valueTable ? valueTable.records : [])
    .filter(v => sameId(v.ticket_id, ticket.id) && String(v.entity_category || 'ticket') === 'ticket' &&
      isActiveFlag(v.is_active) && !v.deleted_at)
    .forEach(v => {
      const option = blank(v.dropdown_option_id) ? null : options[String(v.dropdown_option_id)];
      const raw = [v.number_value, v.text_value, v.date_value, v.dropdown_option_id, v.start_date_value].find(x => !blank(x));
      values[String(v.custom_field_id)] = {
        type: fieldTypes[String(v.custom_field_id)] || '',
        raw: blank(raw) ? '' : raw,
        candidates: [v.text_value, v.number_value, v.date_value, v.dropdown_option_id,
          option && option.value, option && option.label].filter(x => !blank(x)).map(x => String(x).trim())
      };
    });

  Object.keys(STANDARD_CONDITION_FIELDS).forEach(key => {
    const raw = ticket[STANDARD_CONDITION_FIELDS[key]];
    values[key] = { type: 'text', raw: blank(raw) ? '' : raw, candidates: blank(raw) ? [] : [String(raw).trim()] };
  });
  return values;
}

/**
 * Does one condition row hold for the ticket's values? Unknown operators and invalid patterns never match;
 * a missing value only matches is_empty.
 * @param {Object} condition - step_conditions row { custom_field_id, operator, value }
 * @param {Object} values - ticketConditionValues() result
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function conditionMatches(condition, values) {
  const operator = normalizeConditionOperator(condition.operator);
  if (!operator) return false;
  const entry = values[String(condition.custom_field_id)] || { type: '', raw: '', candidates: [] };
  const empty = entry.candidates.length === 0;
  if (operator === 'is_empty') return empty;
  if (operator === 'is_not_empty') return !empty;
  if (empty) return false;

  const expected = String(condition.value === null || condition.value === undefined ? '' : condition.value).trim();
  const lower = s => String(s).toLowerCase();
  const anyEquals = target => entry.candidates.some(c => lower(c) === lower(target));
  const list = expected.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

  switch (operator) {
    case 'equals': return anyEquals(expected);
    case 'not_equals': return !anyEquals(expected);
    case 'contains': return entry.candidates.some(c => lower(c).includes(lower(expected)));
    case 'not_contains': return !entry.candidates.some(c => lower(c).includes(lower(expected)));
    case 'in': return list.some(anyEquals);
    case 'not_in': return !list.some(anyEquals);
    case 'regex_match':
      try {
        const pattern = new RegExp(expected, 'i');
        return entry.candidates.some(c => pattern.test(c));
      } catch (e) {
        return false;
      }
    default: {
      // Ordered comparison: numbers when both sides are numbers, else dates for date fields
      const toNumber = v => {
        const n = Number(String(v).replace(/,/g, ''));
        return String(v).trim() !== '' && isFinite(n) ? n : null;
      };
      let a = toNumber(entry.raw);
      let b = toNumber(expected);
      if (a === null || b === null) {
        if (!/date/.test(entry.type)) return false;
        const da = toDate(entry.raw);
        const db = toDate(expected);
        if (!da || !db) return false;
        a = da.getTime();
        b = db.getTime();
      }
      if (operator === 'greater_than') return a > b;
      if (operator === 'greater_than_or_equal') return a >= b;
      if (operator === 'less_than') return a < b;
      if (operator === 'less_than_or_equal') return a <= b;
      return false;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Where an approved / completed step routes the ticket by its conditions. Groups are tried in order; the
 * first whose conditions all hold wins, else the default route. A target must be a later active step of
 * the workflow (or the end) - anything else is skipped, so a deleted or earlier step can never loop.
 * @param {Object} step - Current step
 * @param {Object} ticket - Ticket record
 * @param {Array} steps - The workflow's active steps in order
 * @returns {Object} { matched, end, step, group, is_default }
 */
function routeByConditions(step, ticket, steps) {
  const groups = stepConditionGroups(step.id);
  if (groups.length === 0) return { matched: false };

  const currentIndex = steps.findIndex(s => sameId(s.id, step.id));
  const targetOf = id => {
    if (id === STEP_CONDITION_END) return { end: true, step: null };
    const index = steps.findIndex(s => sameId(s.id, id));
    return index > currentIndex ? { end: false, step: steps[index] } : null;
  };

  let values = null;
  let fallback = null;
  for (const g of groups) {
    const target = targetOf(g.next_step_id);
    if (!target) continue;
    if (g.is_default) {
      fallback = fallback || { matched: true, ...target, group: g.group, is_default: true };
      continue;
    }
    values = values || ticketConditionValues(ticket);
    if (g.rows.every(row => conditionMatches(row, values))) {
      return { matched: true, ...target, group: g.group, is_default: false };
    }
  }
  return fallback || { matched: false };
}

/**
 * The step a return goes back to: the step the ticket came from on its way to the current step, rebuilt
 * from ticket_history (so a return follows the branch the ticket took, not the step order).
 * @returns {Object} { found, step } - found false when history doesn't show the path (caller falls back)
 */
function previousVisitedStep(ticketId, currentStepId, steps) {
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
  const path = [];
  (table ? table.records : []).filter(h => sameId(h.ticket_id, ticketId)).forEach(h => {
    const to = h.to_step_id === null || h.to_step_id === undefined ? '' : String(h.to_step_id);
    switch (h.action) {
      case 'WORKFLOW_STARTED':
        path.length = 0;
        if (to) path.push(to);
        break;
      case 'APPROVED':
      case 'COMPLETED':
        if (to) path.push(to);
        break;
      case 'RETURNED':
      case 'REOPENED': {
        if (!to) {
          path.length = 0;
          break;
        }
        const at = path.lastIndexOf(to);
        if (at >= 0) path.length = at + 1;
        else path.push(to);
        break;
      }
      default:
        break;
    }
  });

  if (path.length === 0 || path[path.length - 1] !== String(currentStepId)) return { found: false };
  if (path.length === 1) return { found: true, step: null };
  const previous = steps.find(s => sameId(s.id, path[path.length - 2]));
  return previous ? { found: true, step: previous } : { found: false };
}

/**
 * getStepConditions route: a step's routes in the ConditionalWorkflowBuilder shape.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { step_id, enabled, routes: [{ id, conditions: { operator: 'and',
 *   conditions: [{ custom_field_id, operator, value }] }, target_step_id, is_default }] } }
 */
function getStepConditions(stepId) {
  if (!stepId) return { status: 'error', message: 'step_id is required' };
  const routes = stepConditionGroups(stepId).map(g => ({
    id: 'g' + g.group,
    conditions: g.is_default ? null : {
      operator: 'and',
      conditions: g.rows.map(r => ({
        custom_field_id: String(r.custom_field_id),
        operator: normalizeConditionOperator(r.operator) || String(r.operator || ''),
        value: r.value === null || r.value === undefined ? '' : String(r.value)
      }))
    },
    target_step_id: g.next_step_id,
    is_default: g.is_default
  }));
  return { status: 'success', data: { step_id: stepId, enabled: routes.length > 0, routes: routes } };
}

/**
 * saveStepConditions route: replace a step's routes. Refused when a target isn't a later active step of
 * the same workflow (or the end), a field isn't one of the ticket type's fields, an operator is unknown, a
 * value or pattern is missing / invalid, or there is more than one default route.
 * @param {Object} payload - { step_id, enabled, routes, user_id }
 * @returns {Object} getStepConditions() of the saved step
 */
function saveStepConditions(payload) {
  const stepId = payload.step_id || payload.id;
  const found = stepId ? findRecord(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS, stepId) : null;
  if (!found || !isActiveFlag(found.record.is_active) || found.record.deactivated_at) {
    return { status: 'error', message: `Workflow step ${stepId} not found` };
  }
  const step = found.record;
  const stepsResult = getWorkflowSteps(step.ticket_type_id, step.company_id);
  const steps = stepsResult.status === 'success' ? stepsResult.data : [];
  const currentIndex = steps.findIndex(s => sameId(s.id, stepId));

  const fieldTable = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);
  const fieldIds = (fieldTable ? fieldTable.records : [])
    .filter(f => sameId(f.ticket_type_id, step.ticket_type_id) && String(f.entity_category || 'ticket') === 'ticket' &&
      isActiveFlag(f.is_active) && !f.deactivated_at)
    .map(f => String(f.id))
    .concat(Object.keys(STANDARD_CONDITION_FIELDS));

  const routes = boolFlag(payload.enabled === undefined ? true : payload.enabled) && Array.isArray(payload.routes) ? payload.routes : [];
  if (routes.filter(r => boolFlag(r.is_default)).length > 1) {
    return { status: 'error', message: 'Only one default route is allowed' };
  }

  const rowsToWrite = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < routes.length; i++) {
    const route = routes[i];
    const label = `Route ${i + 1}`;
    const target = String(route.target_step_id || '');
    if (target !== STEP_CONDITION_END) {
      const targetIndex = steps.findIndex(s => sameId(s.id, target));
      if (targetIndex === -1) return { status: 'error', message: `${label}: pick a target step of this workflow` };
      if (targetIndex <= currentIndex) return { status: 'error', message: `${label}: the target must be a later step (no loops)` };
    }

    if (boolFlag(route.is_default)) {
      rowsToWrite.push({ group: i + 1, custom_field_id: '', operator: '', value: '', next_step_id: target });
      continue;
    }

    const conditions = route.conditions && Array.isArray(route.conditions.conditions) ? route.conditions.conditions : [];
    if (conditions.length === 0) return { status: 'error', message: `${label}: add at least one condition` };
    for (let j = 0; j < conditions.length; j++) {
      const c = conditions[j];
      const where = `${label}, condition ${j + 1}`;
      const fieldId = String(c.custom_field_id || c.field_id || '');
      if (!fieldIds.includes(fieldId)) return { status: 'error', message: `${where}: pick a field of this ticket type` };
      const operator = normalizeConditionOperator(c.operator);
      if (!operator) return { status: 'error', message: `${where}: unknown operator ${c.operator}` };
      const value = c.value === null || c.value === undefined ? '' : String(c.value).trim();
      if (!value && operator !== 'is_empty' && operator !== 'is_not_empty') {
        return { status: 'error', message: `${where}: a value is required` };
      }
      if (operator === 'regex_match') {
        try {
          new RegExp(value);
        } catch (e) {
          return { status: 'error', message: `${where}: invalid pattern` };
        }
      }
      rowsToWrite.push({ group: i + 1, custom_field_id: fieldId, operator: operator, value: value, next_step_id: target });
    }
  }

  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS);
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.STEP_CONDITIONS);
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();

  table.records.forEach((record, index) => {
    if (!sameId(record.step_id, stepId) || !isActiveFlag(record.is_active) || record.deactivated_at) return;
    const retired = { ...record, is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor, deactivation_reason: 'routes_replaced', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor };
    sheet.getRange(table.rowNumbers[index], 1, 1, table.headers.length).setValues([rowFromRecord(table.headers, retired)]);
  });

  const headers = sheetHeaders(sheet);
  rowsToWrite.forEach(row => {
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(headers, {
      id: generateId('sc'),
      step_id: stepId,
      custom_field_id: row.custom_field_id,
      operator: row.operator,
      value: row.value,
      next_step_id: row.next_step_id,
      condition_group: row.group,
      company_id: step.company_id || '',
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: actor,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: actor
    }));
  });

  logAdminAction(actor, 'SAVE_STEP_CONDITIONS', 'workflow_step', stepId, { routes: routes.length, conditions: rowsToWrite.length });
  const saved = getStepConditions(stepId);
  saved.message = routes.length ? `Saved ${routes.length} route(s)` : 'Conditional routing turned off';
  return saved;
}

// --- SLA status + small lookups ---------------------------------------------------

/** The calendar day of a moment in Philippine time (UTC+8, spec 3.2), as yyyy-MM-dd. */
function phDay(date) {
  return new Date(date.getTime() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/**
 * A ticket's SLA status for reports and the dashboard: Closed, No SLA, Overdue, Due Today or On Track.
 * @returns {string}
 */
function ticketSLAStatus(ticket, now = new Date()) {
  if (TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(ticket.status) || ticket.cancelled_at) return 'Closed';
  const due = toDate(ticket.step_due_date);
  if (!due) return 'No SLA';
  if (due < now) return 'Overdue';
  return phDay(due) === phDay(now) ? 'Due Today' : 'On Track';
}

/** id -> record of a table (empty when the tab doesn't exist). */
function recordsById(sheetName, idColumn = 'id') {
  const table = readTable(sheetName);
  const map = {};
  (table ? table.records : []).forEach(r => { map[String(r[idColumn])] = r; });
  return map;
}

/** A Date / date string as ISO for JSON, '' when blank. */
function isoOrBlank(value) {
  const date = toDate(value);
  return date ? date.toISOString() : '';
}

// --- Reports (spec 3.3 Report Configuration, 3.4 Reporting) ------------------------

/** Standard report columns (field_type 'standard'): field_name -> default display name. */
const STANDARD_REPORT_FIELDS = {
  ticket_number: 'Ticket #',
  title: 'Title',
  status: 'Status',
  priority: 'Priority',
  requester_id: 'Requester',
  company_id: 'Company',
  current_step_name: 'Current Step',
  step_due_date: 'Step Due',
  sla_status: 'SLA Status',
  created_at: 'Created',
  updated_at: 'Updated',
  description: 'Description'
};

/** Columns used when a ticket type has no saved report configuration. */
const DEFAULT_REPORT_FIELDS = ['ticket_number', 'title', 'status', 'current_step_name', 'sla_status', 'created_at'];

/**
 * Every column a report for this ticket type can show: the standard fields, then the type's active ticket
 * custom fields (field_name = custom field id, so a renamed field keeps its column).
 * @returns {Array} [{ field_name, display_name, field_type }]
 */
function availableReportFields(ticketTypeId) {
  const standard = Object.keys(STANDARD_REPORT_FIELDS).map(name => ({ field_name: name, display_name: STANDARD_REPORT_FIELDS[name], field_type: 'standard' }));
  const fieldTable = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);
  const custom = (fieldTable ? fieldTable.records : [])
    .filter(f => sameId(f.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId) && String(f.entity_category || 'ticket') === 'ticket' &&
      isActiveFlag(f.is_active) && !f.deactivated_at)
    .sort((a, b) => (Number(a.sort_order) || 0) - (Number(b.sort_order) || 0))
    .map(f => ({ field_name: String(f.id), display_name: f.label || f.name || String(f.id), field_type: 'custom', custom_type: String(f.type || '') }));
  return standard.concat(custom);
}

/**
 * getReportConfig route: the columns of a ticket type's report. The company's rows win over global rows
 * (blank company_id), like comment requirements; neither = the default columns + every custom field.
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { ticket_type_id, company_id, source, columns, available } }
 */
function getReportConfig(ticketTypeId, companyId = null) {
  if (!ticketTypeId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };
  const available = availableReportFields(ticketTypeId);
  const byName = {};
  available.forEach(f => { byName[f.field_name] = f; });

  const table = readTable(SHEETS.REPORT_CONFIGURATIONS);
  const rows = (table ? table.records : [])
    .filter(r => sameId(r.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId) && isActiveFlag(r.is_active) && !r.deactivated_at && byName[String(r.field_name)]);
  const companyRows = companyId ? rows.filter(r => sameId(r.company_id, companyId)) : [];
  const globalRows = rows.filter(r => !r.company_id);
  const chosen = companyRows.length ? companyRows : globalRows;
  const source = companyRows.length ? 'company' : (globalRows.length ? 'global' : 'default');

  const columns = chosen.length
    ? chosen
      .filter(r => isActiveFlag(r.is_visible))
      .sort((a, b) => (Number(a.sort_order) || 0) - (Number(b.sort_order) || 0))
      .map(r => ({ ...byName[String(r.field_name)], display_name: r.display_name || byName[String(r.field_name)].display_name }))
    : DEFAULT_REPORT_FIELDS.map(name => byName[name]).concat(available.filter(f => f.field_type === 'custom'));

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId, company_id: companyId || '', source: source, columns: columns, available: available }
  };
}

/**
 * saveReportConfig route: replace the report columns of (ticket type, company); blank company_id = the
 * global configuration. Columns must be fields of the ticket type; at least one is required.
 * @param {Object} payload - { ticket_type_id, company_id, columns: [{ field_name, display_name }], user_id }
 * @returns {Object} getReportConfig() after saving
 */
function saveReportConfig(payload) {
  const ticketTypeId = payload.ticket_type_id;
  if (!ticketTypeId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };
  if (!findRecord(SHEETS.TICKET_TYPES, ticketTypeId)) return { status: 'error', message: `Ticket type ${ticketTypeId} not found` };

  const available = availableReportFields(ticketTypeId);
  const byName = {};
  available.forEach(f => { byName[f.field_name] = f; });
  const columns = Array.isArray(payload.columns) ? payload.columns : [];
  if (columns.length === 0) return { status: 'error', message: 'Pick at least one column' };
  const seen = {};
  for (const column of columns) {
    const name = String(column.field_name || '');
    if (!byName[name]) return { status: 'error', message: `Unknown report column: ${name}` };
    if (seen[name]) return { status: 'error', message: `Column ${byName[name].display_name} is listed twice` };
    seen[name] = true;
  }

  const companyId = payload.company_id || '';
  const actor = payload.user_id || getCurrentUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID).getSheetByName(SHEETS.REPORT_CONFIGURATIONS) || ensureTableSheet(SHEETS.REPORT_CONFIGURATIONS);
  const table = readTable(SHEETS.REPORT_CONFIGURATIONS);

  table.records.forEach((record, index) => {
    if (!sameId(record.ticket_type_id, ticketTypeId) || !sameId(record.company_id || '', companyId) ||
      !isActiveFlag(record.is_active) || record.deactivated_at) return;
    const retired = { ...record, is_active: false, deactivated_at: now, deactivated_by: actor, deactivation_reason: 'report_config_replaced', updated_at: now, updated_by: actor };
    sheet.getRange(table.rowNumbers[index], 1, 1, table.headers.length).setValues([rowFromRecord(table.headers, retired)]);
  });

  const headers = sheetHeaders(sheet);
  columns.forEach((column, index) => {
    const field = byName[String(column.field_name)];
    sheet.appendRow(rowFromRecord(headers, {
      id: generateId('rc'),
      ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
      field_name: field.field_name,
      display_name: String(column.display_name || '').trim() || field.display_name,
      field_type: field.field_type,
      sort_order: index + 1,
      is_visible: true,
      is_sortable: true,
      is_filterable: false,
      company_id: companyId,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: actor,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: actor
    }));
  });

  logAdminAction(actor, 'SAVE_REPORT_CONFIG', 'ticket_type', ticketTypeId, { company_id: companyId, columns: columns.map(c => c.field_name) });
  const saved = getReportConfig(ticketTypeId, companyId || null);
  saved.message = `Saved ${columns.length} report column(s)`;
  return saved;
}

/**
 * Display text of each ticket's custom field values: dropdown -> option label, date range -> "start - end".
 * @returns {Object} { [ticketId]: { [customFieldId]: text } }
 */
function customFieldDisplayValues(ticketIds) {
  const wanted = {};
  ticketIds.forEach(id => { wanted[String(id)] = true; });
  const options = recordsById(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_OPTIONS);
  const dateText = v => {
    const date = toDate(v);
    return date ? phDay(date) : String(v);
  };
  const blank = v => v === '' || v === null || v === undefined;

  const out = {};
  const valueTable = readTable(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);
  (valueTable ? valueTable.records : [])
    .filter(v => wanted[String(v.ticket_id)] && String(v.entity_category || 'ticket') === 'ticket' && isActiveFlag(v.is_active) && !v.deleted_at)
    .forEach(v => {
      let text = '';
      if (!blank(v.dropdown_option_id)) {
        const option = options[String(v.dropdown_option_id)];
        text = option ? (option.label || option.value || String(v.dropdown_option_id)) : String(v.dropdown_option_id);
      } else if (!blank(v.start_date_value) || !blank(v.end_date_value)) {
        text = `${blank(v.start_date_value) ? '' : dateText(v.start_date_value)} - ${blank(v.end_date_value) ? '' : dateText(v.end_date_value)}`;
      } else if (!blank(v.date_value)) {
        text = dateText(v.date_value);
      } else if (!blank(v.number_value)) {
        text = String(v.number_value);
      } else {
        text = blank(v.text_value) ? '' : String(v.text_value);
      }
      (out[String(v.ticket_id)] = out[String(v.ticket_id)] || {})[String(v.custom_field_id)] = text;
    });
  return out;
}

/**
 * Ticket ids a non-admin may see in a report: tickets they requested, acted on (ticket_history) or can
 * approve now (the approver inbox).
 * @returns {Object} { [ticketId]: true }
 */
function reportVisibleTicketIds(userId, tickets) {
  const visible = {};
  tickets.forEach(t => { if (sameId(t.requester_id, userId)) visible[String(t.id)] = true; });
  const history = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_HISTORY);
  (history ? history.records : []).forEach(h => { if (sameId(h.user_id, userId)) visible[String(h.ticket_id)] = true; });
  const inbox = getTicketsForApproval(userId);
  if (inbox.status === 'success') inbox.data.forEach(t => { visible[String(t.id)] = true; });
  return visible;
}

/**
 * getReportData route: the report of one ticket type - the configured columns and one row per ticket.
 * Admins see every ticket; others see the tickets they requested, acted on or can approve.
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, ticket_type_id, company_id, status ('open' | 'closed' | a status),
 *   date_from, date_to (created_at, yyyy-MM-dd, inclusive) }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { columns, rows: [{ ticket_id, <field_name>: text }], total, scope } }
 */
function getReportData(params = {}) {
  if (!params.user_id) return { status: 'error', message: 'user_id is required' };
  if (!params.ticket_type_id) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_type_id is required' };

  const config = getReportConfig(params.ticket_type_id, params.company_id || null);
  if (config.status !== 'success') return config;
  const columns = config.data.columns;

  const ticketTable = readTable(SHEETS.TICKETS);
  const from = params.date_from ? toDate(params.date_from) : null;
  const toEnd = params.date_to ? endOfTerm(params.date_to) : null;
  const statusFilter = String(params.status || '').trim();

  let tickets = (ticketTable ? ticketTable.records : []).filter(t => {
    if (!sameId(t.ticket_type_id, params.ticket_type_id) || !isActiveFlag(t.is_active)) return false;
    if (params.company_id && !sameId(t.company_id, params.company_id)) return false;
    const closed = TERMINAL_TICKET_STATUSES.includes(t.status) || Boolean(t.cancelled_at);
    if (statusFilter === 'open' && closed) return false;
    if (statusFilter === 'closed' && !closed) return false;
    if (statusFilter && statusFilter !== 'open' && statusFilter !== 'closed' && String(t.status) !== statusFilter) return false;
    const created = toDate(t.created_at);
    if (from && (!created || created < from)) return false;
    if (toEnd && (!created || created >= toEnd)) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const admin = isAdminUser(params.user_id);
  if (!admin) {
    const visible = reportVisibleTicketIds(params.user_id, tickets);
    tickets = tickets.filter(t => visible[String(t.id)]);
  }

  const steps = recordsById(SHEETS.WORKFLOW_STEPS);
  const companies = recordsById(SHEETS.COMPANIES);
  const profiles = recordsById(SHEETS.USER_PROFILES, 'user_id');
  const customValues = customFieldDisplayValues(tickets.map(t => t.id));
  const now = new Date();

  const standardValue = (ticket, name) => {
    switch (name) {
      case 'current_step_name': return ticket.current_step_id ? ((steps[String(ticket.current_step_id)] || {}).name || '') : '';
      case 'sla_status': return ticketSLAStatus(ticket, now);
      case 'company_id': return (companies[String(ticket.company_id)] || {}).name || String(ticket.company_id || '');
      case 'requester_id': {
        const profile = profiles[String(ticket.requester_id)] || {};
        return profile.display_name || profile.email || String(ticket.requester_id || '');
      }
      case 'step_due_date':
      case 'created_at':
      case 'updated_at': return isoOrBlank(ticket[name]);
      default: return ticket[name] === null || ticket[name] === undefined ? '' : String(ticket[name]);
    }
  };

  const rows = tickets
    .sort((a, b) => (toDate(b.created_at) || 0) - (toDate(a.created_at) || 0))
    .map(ticket => {
      const row = { ticket_id: String(ticket.id) };
      columns.forEach(column => {
        row[column.field_name] = column.field_type === 'custom'
          ? ((customValues[String(ticket.id)] || {})[column.field_name] || '')
          : standardValue(ticket, column.field_name);
      });
      return row;
    });

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: { columns: columns, rows: rows, total: rows.length, scope: admin ? 'all' : 'mine', config_source: config.data.source },
    message: `${rows.length} ticket(s)`
  };
}

// --- Audit log (spec 3.5) ------------------------------------------------------------

/** A log row's details JSON as an object (the text itself when it isn't JSON). */
function parseLogDetails(details) {
  if (details === '' || details === null || details === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof details !== 'string') return details;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(details);
  } catch (e) {
    return details;
  }
}

/**
 * getAdminActionLogs route (admins only): admin_action_logs newest first, filtered and paged.
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, action_type, target_entity, target_id, admin_user_id, date_from,
 *   date_to, limit (default 50, max 500), offset }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: { logs, total, limit, offset, action_types, target_entities } }
 */
function getAdminActionLogs(params = {}) {
  if (!isAdminUser(params.user_id)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only admins can read the audit log' };

  const table = readTable(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTION_LOGS);
  const all = (table ? table.records : []).map((row, index) => ({ row, index }));
  const from = params.date_from ? toDate(params.date_from) : null;
  const toEnd = params.date_to ? endOfTerm(params.date_to) : null;

  const matches = all.filter(({ row }) => {
    if (params.action_type && String(row.action_type) !== String(params.action_type)) return false;
    if (params.target_entity && String(row.target_entity) !== String(params.target_entity)) return false;
    if (params.target_id && !sameId(row.target_id, params.target_id)) return false;
    if (params.admin_user_id && !sameId(row.admin_user_id, params.admin_user_id)) return false;
    const at = toDate(row.timestamp);
    if (from && (!at || at < from)) return false;
    if (toEnd && (!at || at >= toEnd)) return false;
    return true;
  }).sort((a, b) => ((toDate(b.row.timestamp) || 0) - (toDate(a.row.timestamp) || 0)) || (b.index - a.index));

  const limit = Math.min(Math.max(Number(params.limit) || 50, 1), 500);
  const offset = Math.max(Number(params.offset) || 0, 0);
  const logs = matches.slice(offset, offset + limit).map(({ row }) => ({
    ...row,
    timestamp: isoOrBlank(row.timestamp),
    details: parseLogDetails(row.details)
  }));

  return {
    status: 'success',
    data: {
      logs: logs,
      total: matches.length,
      limit: limit,
      offset: offset,
      action_types: unique(all.map(({ row }) => String(row.action_type || '')).filter(Boolean)).sort(),
      target_entities: unique(all.map(({ row }) => String(row.target_entity || '')).filter(Boolean)).sort()
    },
    message: `${matches.length} log entries`
  };
}

/**
 * getTicketActionLogs route (admins only): the system log of one ticket, newest first.
 * @param {Object} params - { user_id, ticket_id }
 * @returns {Object} { status, data: log rows }
 */
function getTicketActionLogs(params = {}) {
  if (!isAdminUser(params.user_id)) return { status: 'error', message: 'Only admins can read the system log' };
  const ticketId = params.ticket_id || params.id;
  if (!ticketId) return { status: 'error', message: 'ticket_id is required' };

  const table = readTable(SHEETS.TICKET_ACTION_LOGS);
  const logs = (table ? table.records : [])
    .map((row, index) => ({ row, index }))
    .filter(({ row }) => sameId(row.ticket_id, ticketId))
    .sort((a, b) => ((toDate(b.row.timestamp) || 0) - (toDate(a.row.timestamp) || 0)) || (b.index - a.index))
    .map(({ row }) => ({ ...row, timestamp: isoOrBlank(row.timestamp), details: parseLogDetails(row.details) }));
  return { status: 'success', data: logs, message: `${logs.length} log entries` };
}
